const MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS: usize = 30;
const MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS: usize = 32;
const MZ_ZIP_CDH_DISK_START_OFS: usize = 34;
const MZ_ZIP_CDH_INTERNAL_ATTR_OFS: usize = 36;
const MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTERNAL_ATTR_OFS: usize = 38;
const MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS: usize = 42;

// ECDH offsets
const MZ_ZIP_ECDH_SIG_OFS: usize = 0;
const MZ_ZIP_ECDH_NUM_THIS_DISK_OFS: usize = 4;
const MZ_ZIP_ECDH_NUM_DISK_CDIR_OFS: usize = 6;
const MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_NUM_ENTRIES_ON_DISK_OFS: usize = 8;
const MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_TOTAL_ENTRIES_OFS: usize = 10;
const MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_SIZE_OFS: usize = 12;
const MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS: usize = 16;
const MZ_ZIP_ECDH_COMMENT_SIZE_OFS: usize = 20;

// ZIP64 ECDL offsets
const MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_SIG_OFS: usize = 0;
const MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_NUM_DISK_CDIR_OFS: usize = 4;
const MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_REL_OFS_TO_ZIP64_ECDR_OFS: usize = 8;
const MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_TOTAL_NUMBER_OF_DISKS_OFS: usize = 16;

// ZIP64 ECDH offsets
const MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_SIG_OFS: usize = 0;
const MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_SIZE_OF_RECORD_OFS: usize = 4;
const MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_VERSION_MADE_BY_OFS: usize = 12;
const MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_VERSION_NEEDED_OFS: usize = 14;
const MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_NUM_THIS_DISK_OFS: usize = 16;
const MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_NUM_DISK_CDIR_OFS: usize = 20;
const MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_NUM_ENTRIES_ON_DISK_OFS: usize = 24;
const MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_TOTAL_ENTRIES_OFS: usize = 32;
const MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_SIZE_OFS: usize = 40;
const MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS: usize = 48;

// Bit flags
const MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_IS_ENCRYPTED: u16 = 1;
const MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_USES_STRONG_ENCRYPTION: u16 = 64;
const MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_COMPRESSED_PATCH_FLAG: u16 = 32;
const MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_LOCAL_DIR_IS_MASKED: u16 = 8192;

// Compression methods
const MZ_DEFLATED: u16 = 8;
const MZ_STORED: u16 = 0;

// Other constants
const MZ_UINT32_MAX: u64 = 0xFFFFFFFF;
const MZ_UINT16_MAX: u32 = 0xFFFF;

// Error type
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum ZipError {
    NotAnArchive,
    FailedFindingCentralDir,
    FileReadFailed,
    InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted,
    UnsupportedMultidisk,
    TooManyFiles,
    UnsupportedCdirSize,
    AllocFailed,
    FileCloseFailed,
    InvalidParameter,
    FileOpenFailed,
    FileSeekFailed,
    UnsupportedMethod,
    UnsupportedEncryption,
    UnsupportedFeature,
    Other(String),
}

impl std::fmt::Display for ZipError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            ZipError::NotAnArchive => write!(f, "Not a valid ZIP archive"),
            ZipError::FailedFindingCentralDir => write!(f, "Failed to find central directory"),
            ZipError::FileReadFailed => write!(f, "File read failed"),
            ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted => write!(f, "Invalid header or corrupted archive"),
            ZipError::UnsupportedMultidisk => write!(f, "Multidisk archives not supported"),
            ZipError::TooManyFiles => write!(f, "Too many files"),
            ZipError::UnsupportedCdirSize => write!(f, "Unsupported central directory size"),
            ZipError::AllocFailed => write!(f, "Memory allocation failed"),
            ZipError::FileCloseFailed => write!(f, "File close failed"),
            ZipError::InvalidParameter => write!(f, "Invalid parameter"),
            ZipError::FileOpenFailed => write!(f, "File open failed"),
            ZipError::FileSeekFailed => write!(f, "File seek failed"),
            ZipError::UnsupportedMethod => write!(f, "Unsupported compression method"),
            ZipError::UnsupportedEncryption => write!(f, "Unsupported encryption"),
            ZipError::UnsupportedFeature => write!(f, "Unsupported feature"),
            ZipError::Other(msg) => write!(f, "{}", msg),
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for ZipError {}

type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, ZipError>;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub enum ZipType {
    Memory,
    File,
    CFile,
    User,
    Invalid,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct ZipInternalState {
    central_dir: Vec<u8>,
    central_dir_offsets: Vec<u32>,
    sorted_central_dir_offsets: Vec<u32>,
    init_flags: u32,
    zip64: bool,
    zip64_has_extended_info_fields: bool,
    file: Option<File>,
    file_archive_start_ofs: u64,
    mem_buffer: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    mem_size: usize,
    mem_capacity: usize,
}

impl ZipInternalState {
    fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            central_dir: Vec::new(),
            central_dir_offsets: Vec::new(),
            sorted_central_dir_offsets: Vec::new(),
            init_flags: 0,
            zip64: false,
            zip64_has_extended_info_fields: false,
            file: None,
            file_archive_start_ofs: 0,
            mem_buffer: None,
            mem_size: 0,
            mem_capacity: 0,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct ZipArchive {
    archive_size: u64,
    total_files: u32,
    central_directory_file_ofs: u64,
    zip_type: ZipType,
    state: Option<Box<ZipInternalState>>,
    last_error: Option<ZipError>,
}

impl ZipArchive {
    fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            archive_size: 0,
            total_files: 0,
            central_directory_file_ofs: 0,
            zip_type: ZipType::Invalid,
            state: None,
            last_error: None,
        }
    }

    fn set_error(&mut self, error: ZipError) -> bool {
        self.last_error = Some(error);
        false
    }

    fn reader_init_internal(&mut self, _flags: u32) -> Result<()> {
        // Initialize basic state
        self.state = Some(Box::new(ZipInternalState::new()));
        Ok(())
    }

    fn reader_read_central_dir(&mut self, flags: u32) -> Result<()> {
        let mut cdir_size = 0u32;
        let mut cdir_entries_on_this_disk = 0u32;
        let mut num_this_disk = 0u32;
        let mut cdir_disk_index = 0u32;
        let mut cdir_ofs = 0u64;
        let mut eocd_ofs = 0u64;
        let mut archive_ofs = 0u64;
        
        let sort_central_dir = (flags & 0x100) == 0; // MZ_ZIP_FLAG_DO_NOT_SORT_CENTRAL_DIRECTORY
        
        // Basic sanity checks
        if self.archive_size < MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 {
            return Err(ZipError::NotAnArchive);
        }

        // Locate end of central directory
        let cur_file_ofs = self.locate_header_sig(MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG, MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE)
            .ok_or(ZipError::FailedFindingCentralDir)?;
        
        eocd_ofs = cur_file_ofs;
        
        // Read EOCD
        let mut eocd_buf = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
        self.read_at(cur_file_ofs, &mut eocd_buf)?;
        
        if read_le_u32(&eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_SIG_OFS..]) != MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
            return Err(ZipError::NotAnArchive);
        }

        // Check for ZIP64 locator
        let state = self.state.as_mut().unwrap();
        if cur_file_ofs >= (MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE + MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE) as u64 {
            let mut zip64_locator = [0u8; MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE];
            let locator_ofs = cur_file_ofs - MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE as u64;
            if self.read_at(locator_ofs, &mut zip64_locator).is_ok() {
                if read_le_u32(&zip64_locator[MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_SIG_OFS..]) == MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIG {
                    state.zip64 = true;
                }
            }
        }

        let mut zip64_end_of_central_dir_ofs = 0u64;
        if state.zip64 {
            // Try locating EOCD64 before the locator
            if cur_file_ofs < (MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE + MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE) as u64 {
                return Err(ZipError::NotAnArchive);
            }

            zip64_end_of_central_dir_ofs = cur_file_ofs - 
                MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE as u64 - 
                MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64;

            // Read ZIP64 EOCD
            let mut zip64_eocd_buf = [0u8; MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
            if self.read_at(zip64_end_of_central_dir_ofs, &mut zip64_eocd_buf).is_err() ||
               read_le_u32(&zip64_eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_SIG_OFS..]) != MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
                
                // Try reading where locator tells us
                let mut zip64_locator = [0u8; MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE];
                let locator_ofs = cur_file_ofs - MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE as u64;
                self.read_at(locator_ofs, &mut zip64_locator)?;
                
                zip64_end_of_central_dir_ofs = read_le_u64(&zip64_locator[MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_REL_OFS_TO_ZIP64_ECDR_OFS..]);
                
                if zip64_end_of_central_dir_ofs > self.archive_size - MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 {
                    return Err(ZipError::NotAnArchive);
                }
                
                self.read_at(zip64_end_of_central_dir_ofs, &mut zip64_eocd_buf)?;
                if read_le_u32(&zip64_eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_SIG_OFS..]) != MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
                    return Err(ZipError::NotAnArchive);
                }
            }
        }

        // Read values from EOCD
        self.total_files = read_le_u16(&eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_TOTAL_ENTRIES_OFS..]) as u32;
        cdir_entries_on_this_disk = read_le_u16(&eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_NUM_ENTRIES_ON_DISK_OFS..]) as u32;
        num_this_disk = read_le_u16(&eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_NUM_THIS_DISK_OFS..]) as u32;
        cdir_disk_index = read_le_u16(&eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_NUM_DISK_CDIR_OFS..]) as u32;
        cdir_size = read_le_u32(&eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_SIZE_OFS..]);
        cdir_ofs = read_le_u32(&eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS..]) as u64;

        // Handle ZIP64 values
        if state.zip64 {
            let mut zip64_locator = [0u8; MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE];
            let locator_ofs = cur_file_ofs - MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE as u64;
            self.read_at(locator_ofs, &mut zip64_locator)?;
            
            let mut zip64_eocd_buf = [0u8; MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
            self.read_at(zip64_end_of_central_dir_ofs, &mut zip64_eocd_buf)?;
            
            let zip64_total_num_of_disks = read_le_u32(&zip64_locator[MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_TOTAL_NUMBER_OF_DISKS_OFS..]);
            let zip64_cdir_total_entries = read_le_u64(&zip64_eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_TOTAL_ENTRIES_OFS..]);
            let zip64_cdir_total_entries_on_this_disk = read_le_u64(&zip64_eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_NUM_ENTRIES_ON_DISK_OFS..]);
            let zip64_size_of_end_of_central_dir_record = read_le_u64(&zip64_eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_SIZE_OF_RECORD_OFS..]);
            let zip64_size_of_central_directory = read_le_u64(&zip64_eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_SIZE_OFS..]);

            if zip64_size_of_end_of_central_dir_record < (MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE - 12) as u64 {
                return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
            }

            if zip64_total_num_of_disks != 1 {
                return Err(ZipError::UnsupportedMultidisk);
            }

            if zip64_cdir_total_entries > MZ_UINT32_MAX {
                return Err(ZipError::TooManyFiles);
            }

            self.total_files = zip64_cdir_total_entries as u32;

            if zip64_cdir_total_entries_on_this_disk > MZ_UINT32_MAX {
                return Err(ZipError::TooManyFiles);
            }

            cdir_entries_on_this_disk = zip64_cdir_total_entries_on_this_disk as u32;

            if zip64_size_of_central_directory > MZ_UINT32_MAX {
                return Err(ZipError::UnsupportedCdirSize);
            }

            cdir_size = zip64_size_of_central_directory as u32;
            num_this_disk = read_le_u32(&zip64_eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_NUM_THIS_DISK_OFS..]);
            cdir_disk_index = read_le_u32(&zip64_eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_NUM_DISK_CDIR_OFS..]);
            cdir_ofs = read_le_u64(&zip64_eocd_buf[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS..]);
        }

        // Validation
        if self.total_files != cdir_entries_on_this_disk {
            return Err(ZipError::UnsupportedMultidisk);
        }

        if (num_this_disk | cdir_disk_index) != 0 && (num_this_disk != 1 || cdir_disk_index != 1) {
            return Err(ZipError::UnsupportedMultidisk);
        }

        if cdir_size < (self.total_files as u64 * MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64) {
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
        }

        if cdir_ofs + cdir_size as u64 > self.archive_size {
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
        }

        if eocd_ofs < cdir_ofs + cdir_size as u64 {
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
        }

        archive_ofs = eocd_ofs - (cdir_ofs + cdir_size as u64);
        if state.zip64 {
            if archive_ofs < (MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE) as u64 {
                return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
            }
            archive_ofs -= (MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE) as u64;
        }

        // Update archive start position if not specified
        if matches!(self.zip_type, ZipType::File | ZipType::CFile | ZipType::User) && state.file_archive_start_ofs == 0 {
            state.file_archive_start_ofs = archive_ofs;
            self.archive_size -= archive_ofs;
        }

        self.central_directory_file_ofs = cdir_ofs;

        // Read central directory
        if self.total_files > 0 {
            let state = self.state.as_mut().unwrap();
            
            // Allocate buffers
            state.central_dir = vec![0u8; cdir_size as usize];
            state.central_dir_offsets = vec![0u32; self.total_files as usize];
            
            if sort_central_dir {
                state.sorted_central_dir_offsets = vec![0u32; self.total_files as usize];
            }

            // Read central directory
            self.read_at(cdir_ofs, &mut state.central_dir)?;

            // Parse central directory entries
            let mut p = &state.central_dir[..];
            let mut remaining = cdir_size as usize;
            
            for i in 0..self.total_files as usize {
                if remaining < MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE {
                    return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                }

                if read_le_u32(p) != MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
                    return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                }

                state.central_dir_offsets[i] = (cdir_size as usize - remaining) as u32;
                
                if sort_central_dir {
                    state.sorted_central_dir_offsets[i] = i as u32;
                }

                let comp_size = read_le_u32(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..]) as u64;
                let decomp_size = read_le_u32(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..]) as u64;
                let local_header_ofs = read_le_u32(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS..]) as u64;
                let filename_size = read_le_u16(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
                let ext_data_size = read_le_u16(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
                let comment_size = read_le_u16(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;

                let total_header_size = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_size + ext_data_size + comment_size;
                
                if total_header_size > remaining {
                    return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                }

                // Check for ZIP64 extended info
                if !state.zip64_has_extended_info_fields && ext_data_size > 0 {
                    if comp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX || decomp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX || local_header_ofs == MZ_UINT32_MAX {
                        // Parse extra data to find ZIP64 extended info
                        let extra_data_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_size;
                        if extra_data_start + ext_data_size <= p.len() {
                            let extra_data = &p[extra_data_start..extra_data_start + ext_data_size];
                            if parse_extra_data_for_zip64(extra_data) {
                                state.zip64 = true;
                                state.zip64_has_extended_info_fields = true;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Validate compression
                let method = read_le_u16(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS..]);
                if method == MZ_STORED && decomp_size != comp_size {
                    return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                }

                let disk_index = read_le_u16(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_DISK_START_OFS..]);
                if disk_index == MZ_UINT16_MAX as u16 || (disk_index != num_this_disk as u16 && disk_index != 1) {
                    return Err(ZipError::UnsupportedMultidisk);
                }

                if comp_size != MZ_UINT32_MAX {
                    if local_header_ofs + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 + comp_size > self.archive_size {
                        return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                    }
                }

                let bit_flags = read_le_u16(&p[MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS..]);
                if bit_flags & MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_LOCAL_DIR_IS_MASKED != 0 {
                    return Err(ZipError::UnsupportedEncryption);
                }

                // Advance to next entry
                p = &p[total_header_size..];
                remaining -= total_header_size;
            }
        }

        // Sort central directory if requested
        if sort_central_dir {
            self.sort_central_dir_offsets_by_filename();
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    fn locate_header_sig(&self, sig: u32, header_size: usize) -> Option<u64> {
        // Simplified implementation - in real code this would search backwards
        // from the end of the archive for the signature
        Some(self.archive_size - header_size as u64)
    }

    fn read_at(&self, offset: u64, buf: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize> {
        match &self.zip_type {
            ZipType::Memory => {
                let state = self.state.as_ref().unwrap();
                if let Some(mem) = &state.mem_buffer {
                    if offset >= mem.len() as u64 {
                        return Ok(0);
                    }
                    let to_read = (mem.len() as u64 - offset).min(buf.len() as u64) as usize;
                    buf[..to_read].copy_from_slice(&mem[offset as usize..offset as usize + to_read]);
                    Ok(to_read)
                } else {
                    Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter)
                }
            }
            ZipType::File | ZipType::CFile => {
                let state = self.state.as_ref().unwrap();
                if let Some(mut file) = state.file.as_ref() {
                    let actual_offset = offset + state.file_archive_start_ofs;
                    if let Err(_) = file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(actual_offset)) {
                        return Err(ZipError::FileSeekFailed);
                    }
                    match file.read(buf) {
                        Ok(n) => Ok(n),
                        Err(_) => Err(ZipError::FileReadFailed),
                    }
                } else {
                    Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter)
                }
            }
            _ => Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter),
        }
    }

    fn sort_central_dir_offsets_by_filename(&mut self) {
        // Simplified implementation
        let state = self.state.as_mut().unwrap();
        state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.sort_by(|a, b| {
            let a_offset = state.central_dir_offsets[*a as usize] as usize;
            let b_offset = state.central_dir_offsets[*b as usize] as usize;
            
            let a_name_len = read_le_u16(&state.central_dir[a_offset + MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
            let b_name_len = read_le_u16(&state.central_dir[b_offset + MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
            
            let a_name = &state.central_dir[a_offset + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE..a_offset + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + a_name_len];
            let b_name = &state.central_dir[b_offset + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE..b_offset + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + b_name_len];
            
            a_name.cmp(b_name)
        });
    }

    fn get_cdh(&self, file_index: u32) -> Result<&[u8]> {
        let state = self.state.as_ref().ok_or(ZipError::InvalidParameter)?;
        
        if file_index >= self.total_files {
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }
        
        let offset = state.central_dir_offsets[file_index as usize] as usize;
        if offset >= state.central_dir.len() {
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
        }
        
        Ok(&state.central_dir[offset..])
    }

    pub fn zero_struct(&mut self) {
        *self = ZipArchive::new();
    }

    fn reader_end_internal(&mut self, set_last_error: bool) -> Result<()> {
        if self.state.is_none() {
            if set_last_error {
                self.last_error = Some(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
            }
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }

        // Close file if open
        if let Some(state) = self.state.take() {
            if let Some(file) = state.file {
                if let Err(_) = drop(file) {
                    if set_last_error {
                        self.last_error = Some(ZipError::FileCloseFailed);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        self.zip_type = ZipType::Invalid;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn reader_end(&mut self) -> Result<()> {
        self.reader_end_internal(true)
    }

    pub fn reader_init(&mut self, size: u64, flags: u32) -> Result<()> {
        self.reader_init_internal(flags)?;
        self.zip_type = ZipType::User;
        self.archive_size = size;
        self.reader_read_central_dir(flags)
    }

    pub fn reader_init_mem(&mut self, p_mem: &[u8], flags: u32) -> Result<()> {
        if p_mem.len() < MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE {
            return Err(ZipError::NotAnArchive);
        }

        self.reader_init_internal(flags)?;
        self.zip_type = ZipType::Memory;
        self.archive_size = p_mem.len() as u64;
        
        let state = self.state.as_mut().unwrap();
        state.mem_buffer = Some(p_mem.to_vec());
        state.mem_size = p_mem.len();
        state.mem_capacity = p_mem.len();

        self.reader_read_central_dir(flags)
    }

    pub fn reader_init_file(&mut self, filename: &str, flags: u32) -> Result<()> {
        self.reader_init_file_v2(filename, flags, 0, 0)
    }

    pub fn reader_init_file_v2(
        &mut self,
        filename: &str,
        flags: u32,
        file_start_ofs: u64,
        archive_size: u64,
    ) -> Result<()> {
        if archive_size > 0 && archive_size < MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 {
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }

        let file = File::open(filename).map_err(|_| ZipError::FileOpenFailed)?;
        
        let file_size = if archive_size == 0 {
            file.metadata().map_err(|_| ZipError::FileSeekFailed)?.len()
        } else {
            archive_size
        };

        if file_size < MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 {
            return Err(ZipError::NotAnArchive);
        }

        self.reader_init_internal(flags)?;
        self.zip_type = ZipType::File;
        self.archive_size = file_size;
        
        let state = self.state.as_mut().unwrap();
        state.file = Some(file);
        state.file_archive_start_ofs = file_start_ofs;

        self.reader_read_central_dir(flags)
    }

    pub fn reader_init_cfile(
        &mut self,
        file: File,
        archive_size: u64,
        flags: u32,
    ) -> Result<()> {
        let cur_file_ofs = file.seek(SeekFrom::Current(0)).map_err(|_| ZipError::FileSeekFailed)?;
        
        let actual_archive_size = if archive_size == 0 {
            let end_pos = file.seek(SeekFrom::End(0)).map_err(|_| ZipError::FileSeekFailed)?;
            file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(cur_file_ofs)).map_err(|_| ZipError::FileSeekFailed)?;
            end_pos - cur_file_ofs
        } else {
            archive_size
        };

        if actual_archive_size < MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 {
            return Err(ZipError::NotAnArchive);
        }

        self.reader_init_internal(flags)?;
        self.zip_type = ZipType::CFile;
        self.archive_size = actual_archive_size;
        
        let state = self.state.as_mut().unwrap();
        state.file = Some(file);
        state.file_archive_start_ofs = cur_file_ofs;

        self.reader_read_central_dir(flags)
    }

    pub fn reader_is_file_encrypted(&self, file_index: u32) -> Result<bool> {
        let cdh = self.get_cdh(file_index)?;
        
        let bit_flag = read_le_u16(&cdh[MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS..]);
        Ok(bit_flag & (MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_IS_ENCRYPTED | MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_USES_STRONG_ENCRYPTION) != 0)
    }

    pub fn reader_is_file_supported(&self, file_index: u32) -> Result<bool> {
        let cdh = self.get_cdh(file_index)?;
        
        let method = read_le_u16(&cdh[MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS..]);
        let bit_flag = read_le_u16(&cdh[MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS..]);

        if method != MZ_STORED && method != MZ_DEFLATED {
            return Err(ZipError::UnsupportedMethod);
        }

        if bit_flag & (MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_IS_ENCRYPTED | MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_USES_STRONG_ENCRYPTION) != 0 {
            return Err(ZipError::UnsupportedEncryption);
        }

        if bit_flag & MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_COMPRESSED_PATCH_FLAG != 0 {
            return Err(ZipError::UnsupportedFeature);
        }

        Ok(true)
    }
}

// Helper functions for reading little-endian values
fn read_le_u16(data: &[u8]) -> u16 {
    u16::from_le_bytes([data[0], data[1]])
}

fn read_le_u32(data: &[u8]) -> u32 {
    u32::from_le_bytes([data[0], data[1], data[2], data[3]])
}

fn read_le_u64(data: &[u8]) -> u64 {
    u64::from_le_bytes([
        data[0], data[1], data[2], data[3],
        data[4], data[5], data[6], data[7],
    ])
}

fn parse_extra_data_for_zip64(extra_data: &[u8]) -> bool {
    let mut pos = 0;
    while pos + 4 <= extra_data.len() {
        let header_id = read_le_u16(&extra_data[pos..]);
        let data_size = read_le_u16(&extra_data[pos + 2..]) as usize;
        
        if header_id == MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID {
            return true;
        }
        
        pos += 4 + data_size;
    }
    false
}

// --- Module: mz_p3 ---
use std::{
    cmp::Ordering,
    convert::TryFrom,
    fmt,
    io::{self, Read, Seek, SeekFrom},
    mem,
    slice,
    time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH},
};

// Constants from miniz.h


// Offsets within central directory header

// Offsets within local directory header
const MZ_ZIP_LDH_SIG_OFS: usize = 0;
const MZ_ZIP_LDH_VERSION_NEEDED_OFS: usize = 4;
const MZ_ZIP_LDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS: usize = 6;
const MZ_ZIP_LDH_METHOD_OFS: usize = 8;
const MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILE_TIME_OFS: usize = 10;
const MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILE_DATE_OFS: usize = 12;
const MZ_ZIP_LDH_CRC32_OFS: usize = 14;
const MZ_ZIP_LDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS: usize = 18;
const MZ_ZIP_LDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS: usize = 22;
const MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS: usize = 26;
const MZ_ZIP_LDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS: usize = 28;

const MZ_ZIP_DOS_DIR_ATTRIBUTE_BITFLAG: u32 = 0x10;
const MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_UTF8: u16 = 1 << 11;

const MZ_ZIP_MAX_ARCHIVE_FILENAME_SIZE: usize = 512;
const MZ_ZIP_MAX_ARCHIVE_FILE_COMMENT_SIZE: usize = 512;
const MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE: usize = 1024 * 64;


// Error type for ZIP operations
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]

impl fmt::Display for ZipError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        match self {
            ZipError::InvalidParameter => write!(f, "Invalid parameter"),
            ZipError::FileNotFound => write!(f, "File not found"),
            ZipError::InvalidHeader => write!(f, "Invalid header or corrupted archive"),
            ZipError::UnsupportedMethod => write!(f, "Unsupported compression method"),
            ZipError::UnsupportedEncryption => write!(f, "Unsupported encryption"),
            ZipError::BufTooSmall => write!(f, "Buffer too small"),
            ZipError::FileReadFailed => write!(f, "File read failed"),
            ZipError::CrcCheckFailed => write!(f, "CRC check failed"),
            ZipError::DecompressionFailed => write!(f, "Decompression failed"),
            ZipError::UnexpectedDecompressedSize => write!(f, "Unexpected decompressed size"),
            ZipError::InternalError => write!(f, "Internal error"),
            ZipError::AllocFailed => write!(f, "Allocation failed"),
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for ZipError {}

type ZipResult<T> = Result<T, ZipError>;

// Simple wrapper for result with archive error state

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum ZipMode {
    Reading,
    Writing,
    Invalid,
}


impl<R: Read + Seek> ZipArchive<R> {
    pub fn new(reader: R) -> Self {
        Self {
            reader,
            state: ZipInternalState {
                central_dir: Vec::new(),
                central_dir_offsets: Vec::new(),
                sorted_central_dir_offsets: Vec::new(),
                init_flags: 0,
                zip64: false,
                zip64_has_extended_info_fields: false,
                archive_start_ofs: 0,
                mem_size: 0,
                mem_capacity: 0,
            },
            total_files: 0,
            archive_size: 0,
            zip_mode: ZipMode::Invalid,
            last_error: None,
        }
    }

    fn set_error(&mut self, error: ZipError) -> bool {
        self.last_error = Some(error);
        false
    }

    fn read_le_u16(&mut self, offset: u64) -> io::Result<u16> {
        let mut buf = [0u8; 2];
        self.reader.seek(SeekFrom::Start(offset))?;
        self.reader.read_exact(&mut buf)?;
        Ok(u16::from_le_bytes(buf))
    }

    fn read_le_u32(&mut self, offset: u64) -> io::Result<u32> {
        let mut buf = [0u8; 4];
        self.reader.seek(SeekFrom::Start(offset))?;
        self.reader.read_exact(&mut buf)?;
        Ok(u32::from_le_bytes(buf))
    }

    fn read_le_u64(&mut self, offset: u64) -> io::Result<u64> {
        let mut buf = [0u8; 8];
        self.reader.seek(SeekFrom::Start(offset))?;
        self.reader.read_exact(&mut buf)?;
        Ok(u64::from_le_bytes(buf))
    }

    fn read_bytes(&mut self, offset: u64, len: usize) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>> {
        let mut buf = vec![0u8; len];
        self.reader.seek(SeekFrom::Start(offset))?;
        self.reader.read_exact(&mut buf)?;
        Ok(buf)
    }

    fn get_cdh(&mut self, file_index: u32) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
        if file_index >= self.total_files {
            return None;
        }

        let offset = self.state.central_dir_offsets.get(file_index as usize)?;
        let start = *offset as usize;
        
        // Read the fixed-size part first to get variable lengths
        if start + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE > self.state.central_dir.len() {
            return None;
        }
        
        let header_slice = &self.state.central_dir[start..start + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
        
        let filename_len = u16::from_le_bytes(
            header_slice[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS + 2]
                .try_into()
                .unwrap(),
        ) as usize;
        
        let extra_len = u16::from_le_bytes(
            header_slice[MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS + 2]
                .try_into()
                .unwrap(),
        ) as usize;
        
        let comment_len = u16::from_le_bytes(
            header_slice[MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS + 2]
                .try_into()
                .unwrap(),
        ) as usize;
        
        let total_len = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_len + extra_len + comment_len;
        let end = start + total_len;
        
        if end > self.state.central_dir.len() {
            return None;
        }
        
        Some(self.state.central_dir[start..end].to_vec())
    }

    pub fn is_file_a_directory(&mut self, file_index: u32) -> ZipResult<bool> {
        let cdh_bytes = self
            .get_cdh(file_index)
            .ok_or(ZipError::InvalidParameter)?;

        let filename_len = u16::from_le_bytes(
            cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS + 2]
                .try_into()
                .unwrap(),
        ) as usize;

        if filename_len > 0 {
            let filename_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE;
            let filename_end = filename_start + filename_len;
            
            if cdh_bytes[filename_end - 1] == b'/' {
                return Ok(true);
            }
        }

        let external_attr = u32::from_le_bytes(
            cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTERNAL_ATTR_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTERNAL_ATTR_OFS + 4]
                .try_into()
                .unwrap(),
        );

        if external_attr & MZ_ZIP_DOS_DIR_ATTRIBUTE_BITFLAG != 0 {
            return Ok(true);
        }

        Ok(false)
    }

    pub fn file_stat(
        &mut self,
        file_index: u32,
    ) -> ZipResult<(ZipFileStat, bool)> {
        let cdh_bytes = self
            .get_cdh(file_index)
            .ok_or(ZipError::InvalidParameter)?;

        self.file_stat_internal(file_index, &cdh_bytes)
    }

    fn file_stat_internal(
        &mut self,
        file_index: u32,
        cdh_bytes: &[u8],
    ) -> ZipResult<(ZipFileStat, bool)> {
        if cdh_bytes.len() < MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE {
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }

        let mut stat = ZipFileStat {
            file_index,
            central_dir_ofs: self
                .state
                .central_dir_offsets
                .get(file_index as usize)
                .copied()
                .unwrap_or(0),
            version_made_by: u16::from_le_bytes(
                cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_VERSION_MADE_BY_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_VERSION_MADE_BY_OFS + 2]
                    .try_into()
                    .unwrap(),
            ),
            version_needed: u16::from_le_bytes(
                cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_VERSION_NEEDED_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_VERSION_NEEDED_OFS + 2]
                    .try_into()
                    .unwrap(),
            ),
            bit_flag: u16::from_le_bytes(
                cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS + 2]
                    .try_into()
                    .unwrap(),
            ),
            method: u16::from_le_bytes(
                cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS + 2]
                    .try_into()
                    .unwrap(),
            ),
            crc32: u32::from_le_bytes(
                cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_CRC32_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_CRC32_OFS + 4]
                    .try_into()
                    .unwrap(),
            ),
            comp_size: u32::from_le_bytes(
                cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS + 4]
                    .try_into()
                    .unwrap(),
            ) as u64,
            uncomp_size: u32::from_le_bytes(
                cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS + 4]
                    .try_into()
                    .unwrap(),
            ) as u64,
            internal_attr: u16::from_le_bytes(
                cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_INTERNAL_ATTR_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_INTERNAL_ATTR_OFS + 2]
                    .try_into()
                    .unwrap(),
            ),
            external_attr: u32::from_le_bytes(
                cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTERNAL_ATTR_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTERNAL_ATTR_OFS + 4]
                    .try_into()
                    .unwrap(),
            ),
            local_header_ofs: u32::from_le_bytes(
                cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS + 4]
                    .try_into()
                    .unwrap(),
            ) as u64,
            filename: String::new(),
            comment: Vec::new(),
            is_directory: false,
            is_encrypted: false,
            is_supported: true,
        };

        // Read DOS date/time
        let file_time = u16::from_le_bytes(
            cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILE_TIME_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILE_TIME_OFS + 2]
                .try_into()
                .unwrap(),
        );
        let file_date = u16::from_le_bytes(
            cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILE_DATE_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILE_DATE_OFS + 2]
                .try_into()
                .unwrap(),
        );
        // Note: dos_to_time_t would be implemented separately

        // Copy filename
        let filename_len = u16::from_le_bytes(
            cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS + 2]
                .try_into()
                .unwrap(),
        ) as usize;
        
        let filename_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE;
        let filename_end = filename_start + filename_len.min(MZ_ZIP_MAX_ARCHIVE_FILENAME_SIZE - 1);
        
        if filename_end <= cdh_bytes.len() {
            stat.filename = String::from_utf8_lossy(&cdh_bytes[filename_start..filename_end])
                .trim_end_matches('\0')
                .to_string();
        }

        // Copy comment
        let extra_len = u16::from_le_bytes(
            cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS + 2]
                .try_into()
                .unwrap(),
        ) as usize;
        let comment_len = u16::from_le_bytes(
            cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS + 2]
                .try_into()
                .unwrap(),
        ) as usize;
        
        let comment_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_len + extra_len;
        let comment_end = comment_start + comment_len.min(MZ_ZIP_MAX_ARCHIVE_FILE_COMMENT_SIZE - 1);
        
        if comment_end <= cdh_bytes.len() {
            stat.comment = cdh_bytes[comment_start..comment_end].to_vec();
        }

        // Set convenience flags
        stat.is_directory = self.is_file_a_directory(file_index).unwrap_or(false);
        stat.is_encrypted = (stat.bit_flag
            & (MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_IS_ENCRYPTED
                | MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_USES_STRONG_ENCRYPTION))
            != 0;
        // Simplified: assume deflate and store are supported
        stat.is_supported = stat.method == 0 || stat.method == 8;

        let mut found_zip64 = false;

        // Check for zip64 extended information
        if stat.comp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64
            || stat.uncomp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64
            || stat.local_header_ofs == MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64
        {
            let extra_len = u16::from_le_bytes(
                cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS + 2]
                    .try_into()
                    .unwrap(),
            ) as usize;
            
            if extra_len > 0 {
                let extra_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_len;
                let extra_end = extra_start + extra_len;
                
                if extra_end <= cdh_bytes.len() {
                    let extra_data = &cdh_bytes[extra_start..extra_end];
                    let mut pos = 0;
                    
                    while pos + 4 <= extra_data.len() {
                        let field_id = u16::from_le_bytes(extra_data[pos..pos + 2].try_into().unwrap());
                        let field_size = u16::from_le_bytes(extra_data[pos + 2..pos + 4].try_into().unwrap()) as usize;
                        
                        if pos + 4 + field_size > extra_data.len() {
                            break;
                        }
                        
                        if field_id == MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID {
                            found_zip64 = true;
                            let field_data = &extra_data[pos + 4..pos + 4 + field_size];
                            let mut field_pos = 0;
                            
                            if stat.uncomp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 {
                                if field_pos + 8 <= field_data.len() {
                                    stat.uncomp_size = u64::from_le_bytes(
                                        field_data[field_pos..field_pos + 8].try_into().unwrap(),
                                    );
                                    field_pos += 8;
                                }
                            }
                            
                            if stat.comp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 {
                                if field_pos + 8 <= field_data.len() {
                                    stat.comp_size = u64::from_le_bytes(
                                        field_data[field_pos..field_pos + 8].try_into().unwrap(),
                                    );
                                    field_pos += 8;
                                }
                            }
                            
                            if stat.local_header_ofs == MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 {
                                if field_pos + 8 <= field_data.len() {
                                    stat.local_header_ofs = u64::from_le_bytes(
                                        field_data[field_pos..field_pos + 8].try_into().unwrap(),
                                    );
                                }
                            }
                            
                            break;
                        }
                        
                        pos += 4 + field_size;
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        Ok((stat, found_zip64))
    }

    fn zip_string_equal(p_a: &[u8], p_b: &[u8], flags: u32) -> bool {
        if flags & crate::MZ_ZIP_FLAG_CASE_SENSITIVE != 0 {
            return p_a == p_b;
        }
        
        if p_a.len() != p_b.len() {
            return false;
        }
        
        p_a.iter().zip(p_b.iter()).all(|(a, b)| {
            a.to_ascii_lowercase() == b.to_ascii_lowercase()
        })
    }

    fn filename_compare(
        &self,
        l_index: u32,
        p_r: &[u8],
    ) -> Ordering {
        let Some(cdh_bytes) = self.get_cdh(l_index) else {
            return Ordering::Greater; // Shouldn't happen
        };
        
        let filename_len = u16::from_le_bytes(
            cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS + 2]
                .try_into()
                .unwrap(),
        ) as usize;
        
        let filename_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE;
        let filename_end = filename_start + filename_len.min(p_r.len());
        
        let p_l = &cdh_bytes[filename_start..filename_end];
        
        for (i, (l, r)) in p_l.iter().zip(p_r.iter()).enumerate() {
            let l_lower = l.to_ascii_lowercase();
            let r_lower = r.to_ascii_lowercase();
            
            if l_lower != r_lower {
                return (l_lower as i32).cmp(&(r_lower as i32));
            }
            
            if i >= p_r.len() - 1 {
                break;
            }
        }
        
        (filename_len as i32).cmp(&(p_r.len() as i32))
    }

    fn locate_file_binary_search(&mut self, filename: &str) -> ZipResult<Option<u32>> {
        let filename_bytes = filename.as_bytes();
        
        if self.state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.is_empty() {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        
        let mut l = 0i64;
        let mut h = (self.total_files as i64) - 1;
        
        while l <= h {
            let m = l + ((h - l) >> 1);
            let file_index = self.state.sorted_central_dir_offsets[m as usize];
            
            match self.filename_compare(file_index, filename_bytes) {
                Ordering::Equal => return Ok(Some(file_index)),
                Ordering::Less => l = m + 1,
                Ordering::Greater => h = m - 1,
            }
        }
        
        Ok(None)
    }

    pub fn locate_file(
        &mut self,
        name: &str,
        comment: Option<&str>,
        flags: u32,
    ) -> ZipResult<Option<i32>> {
        let index = self.locate_file_v2(name, comment, flags)?;
        Ok(index.map(|i| i as i32))
    }

    pub fn locate_file_v2(
        &mut self,
        name: &str,
        comment: Option<&str>,
        flags: u32,
    ) -> ZipResult<Option<u32>> {
        if name.is_empty() {
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }

        let name_len = name.len();
        if name_len > MZ_UINT16_MAX as usize {
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }

        let comment_len = comment.map(|c| c.len()).unwrap_or(0);
        if comment_len > MZ_UINT16_MAX as usize {
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }

        // Try binary search if conditions are met
        if (self.state.init_flags & crate::MZ_ZIP_FLAG_DO_NOT_SORT_CENTRAL_DIRECTORY) == 0
            && self.zip_mode == ZipMode::Reading
            && (flags & (crate::MZ_ZIP_FLAG_IGNORE_PATH | crate::MZ_ZIP_FLAG_CASE_SENSITIVE)) == 0
            && comment.is_none()
            && !self.state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.is_empty()
        {
            return self.locate_file_binary_search(name);
        }

        // Linear search
        let name_bytes = name.as_bytes();
        
        for file_index in 0..self.total_files {
            let Some(cdh_bytes) = self.get_cdh(file_index) else {
                continue;
            };
            
            let filename_len = u16::from_le_bytes(
                cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS + 2]
                    .try_into()
                    .unwrap(),
            ) as usize;
            
            if filename_len < name_len {
                continue;
            }
            
            // Check comment if provided
            if let Some(comment_str) = comment {
                let extra_len = u16::from_le_bytes(
                    cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS + 2]
                        .try_into()
                        .unwrap(),
                ) as usize;
                let file_comment_len = u16::from_le_bytes(
                    cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS..MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS + 2]
                        .try_into()
                        .unwrap(),
                ) as usize;
                
                let comment_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_len + extra_len;
                let comment_end = comment_start + file_comment_len;
                
                if file_comment_len != comment_str.len()
                    || comment_end > cdh_bytes.len()
                    || !Self::zip_string_equal(
                        comment_str.as_bytes(),
                        &cdh_bytes[comment_start..comment_end],
                        flags,
                    )
                {
                    continue;
                }
            }
            
            let mut compare_filename = &cdh_bytes[MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE
                ..MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_len];
            let mut compare_len = filename_len;
            
            // Handle ignore path flag
            if flags & crate::MZ_ZIP_FLAG_IGNORE_PATH != 0 && filename_len > 0 {
                if let Some(pos) = compare_filename.iter().rposition(|&c| {
                    c == b'/' || c == b'\\' || c == b':'
                }) {
                    compare_filename = &compare_filename[pos + 1..];
                    compare_len = compare_filename.len();
                }
            }
            
            if compare_len == name_len
                && Self::zip_string_equal(name_bytes, compare_filename, flags)
            {
                return Ok(Some(file_index));
            }
        }
        
        Ok(None)
    }

    pub fn extract_to_mem(
        &mut self,
        file_index: u32,
        buf: &mut [u8],
        flags: u32,
    ) -> ZipResult<usize> {
        self.extract_to_mem_no_alloc1(file_index, buf, flags, None, 0, None)
    }

    pub fn extract_file_to_mem(
        &mut self,
        filename: &str,
        buf: &mut [u8],
        flags: u32,
    ) -> ZipResult<usize> {
        let Some(file_index) = self.locate_file_v2(filename, None, flags)? else {
            return Err(ZipError::FileNotFound);
        };
        
        self.extract_to_mem(file_index, buf, flags)
    }

    pub fn extract_to_heap(
        &mut self,
        file_index: u32,
        flags: u32,
    ) -> ZipResult<Vec<u8>> {
        let (stat, _) = self.file_stat(file_index)?;
        
        let alloc_size = if flags & crate::MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA != 0 {
            stat.comp_size
        } else {
            stat.uncomp_size
        };
        
        if alloc_size > usize::MAX as u64 {
            return Err(ZipError::InternalError);
        }
        
        let mut buf = vec![0u8; alloc_size as usize];
        let extracted = self.extract_to_mem_no_alloc1(
            file_index,
            &mut buf,
            flags,
            None,
            0,
            Some(&stat),
        )?;
        
        if extracted != alloc_size as usize {
            return Err(ZipError::UnexpectedDecompressedSize);
        }
        
        Ok(buf)
    }

    pub fn extract_file_to_heap(
        &mut self,
        filename: &str,
        flags: u32,
    ) -> ZipResult<Vec<u8>> {
        let Some(file_index) = self.locate_file_v2(filename, None, flags)? else {
            return Err(ZipError::FileNotFound);
        };
        
        self.extract_to_heap(file_index, flags)
    }

    fn extract_to_mem_no_alloc1(
        &mut self,
        file_index: u32,
        buf: &mut [u8],
        flags: u32,
        user_read_buf: Option<&mut [u8]>,
        user_read_buf_size: usize,
        st: Option<&ZipFileStat>,
    ) -> ZipResult<usize> {
        let stat = if let Some(s) = st {
            *s
        } else {
            let (s, _) = self.file_stat(file_index)?;
            s
        };

        // Check for directory or zero-length file
        if stat.is_directory || stat.comp_size == 0 {
            return Ok(0);
        }

        // Check encryption
        if stat.bit_flag
            & (MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_IS_ENCRYPTED
                | MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_USES_STRONG_ENCRYPTION
                | MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_COMPRESSED_PATCH_FLAG)
            != 0
        {
            return Err(ZipError::UnsupportedEncryption);
        }

        // Check compression method
        if (flags & crate::MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0
            && stat.method != 0
            && stat.method != 8
        {
            return Err(ZipError::UnsupportedMethod);
        }

        // Check buffer size
        let needed_size = if flags & crate::MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA != 0 {
            stat.comp_size
        } else {
            stat.uncomp_size
        };
        
        if buf.len() < needed_size as usize {
            return Err(ZipError::BufTooSmall);
        }

        // Read and parse local header
        let mut local_header = vec![0u8; MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
        self.reader.seek(SeekFrom::Start(stat.local_header_ofs))?;
        self.reader.read_exact(&mut local_header)?;
        
        let sig = u32::from_le_bytes(local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_SIG_OFS..MZ_ZIP_LDH_SIG_OFS + 4]
            .try_into()
            .unwrap());
        
        if sig != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeader);
        }
        
        let filename_len = u16::from_le_bytes(
            local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS + 2]
                .try_into()
                .unwrap(),
        ) as u64;
        let extra_len = u16::from_le_bytes(
            local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..MZ_ZIP_LDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS + 2]
                .try_into()
                .unwrap(),
        ) as u64;
        
        let data_ofs = stat.local_header_ofs
            + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64
            + filename_len
            + extra_len;
        
        if data_ofs + stat.comp_size > self.archive_size {
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeader);
        }

        // Handle stored data or compressed data requested
        if (flags & crate::MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) != 0 || stat.method == 0 {
            self.reader.seek(SeekFrom::Start(data_ofs))?;
            self.reader.read_exact(&mut buf[..needed_size as usize])?;
            
            // Verify CRC if not compressed data
            if (flags & crate::MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 {
                let crc = crc32(&buf[..stat.uncomp_size as usize]);
                if crc != stat.crc32 {
                    return Err(ZipError::CrcCheckFailed);
                }
            }
            
            return Ok(needed_size as usize);
        }

        // Handle deflated data
        let mut inflator = flate2::Decompress::new(false);
        let mut read_buf = Vec::new();
        let mut using_user_buf = false;
        
        let (mut read_buf_slice, mut comp_remaining) = if let Some(user_buf) = user_read_buf {
            using_user_buf = true;
            if user_read_buf_size == 0 {
                return Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
            }
            (user_buf, stat.comp_size)
        } else {
            let buf_size = stat.comp_size.min(MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE as u64) as usize;
            read_buf = vec![0u8; buf_size];
            (&mut read_buf[..], stat.comp_size)
        };
        
        let mut cur_file_ofs = data_ofs;
        let mut out_buf_ofs = 0;
        let mut read_buf_avail = 0;
        let mut read_buf_ofs = 0;
        
        loop {
            if read_buf_avail == 0 && comp_remaining > 0 {
                let to_read = read_buf_slice.len().min(comp_remaining as usize);
                self.reader.seek(SeekFrom::Start(cur_file_ofs))?;
                let n = self.reader.read(&mut read_buf_slice[..to_read])?;
                
                if n == 0 {
                    break;
                }
                
                read_buf_avail = n;
                read_buf_ofs = 0;
                cur_file_ofs += n as u64;
                comp_remaining -= n as u64;
            }
            
            let in_buf = &read_buf_slice[read_buf_ofs..read_buf_ofs + read_buf_avail];
            let out_buf = &mut buf[out_buf_ofs..];
            
            let result = inflator.decompress(
                in_buf,
                out_buf,
                flate2::FlushDecompress::None,
            );
            
            match result {
                Ok((bytes_in, bytes_out, _)) => {
                    read_buf_avail -= bytes_in;
                    read_buf_ofs += bytes_in;
                    out_buf_ofs += bytes_out;
                    
                    if bytes_out == 0 && comp_remaining == 0 {
                        break;
                    }
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    return Err(ZipError::DecompressionFailed);
                }
            }
        }
        
        if out_buf_ofs != stat.uncomp_size as usize {
            return Err(ZipError::UnexpectedDecompressedSize);
        }
        
        // Verify CRC
        let crc = crc32(&buf[..stat.uncomp_size as usize]);
        if crc != stat.crc32 {
            return Err(ZipError::CrcCheckFailed);
        }
        
        Ok(out_buf_ofs)
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ZipFileStat {
    pub file_index: u32,
    pub central_dir_ofs: u32,
    pub version_made_by: u16,
    pub version_needed: u16,
    pub bit_flag: u16,
    pub method: u16,
    pub crc32: u32,
    pub comp_size: u64,
    pub uncomp_size: u64,
    pub internal_attr: u16,
    pub external_attr: u32,
    pub local_header_ofs: u64,
    pub filename: String,
    pub comment: Vec<u8>,
    pub is_directory: bool,
    pub is_encrypted: bool,
    pub is_supported: bool,
}

// Simple CRC32 implementation (would normally use crc32fast crate)
fn crc32(data: &[u8]) -> u32 {
    let mut crc: u32 = 0xFFFFFFFF;
    
    for &byte in data {
        crc ^= byte as u32;
        for _ in 0..8 {
            crc = if (crc & 1) != 0 {
                (crc >> 1) ^ 0xEDB88320
            } else {
                crc >> 1
            };
        }
    }
    
    !crc
}

// Module-level constants for flags
pub const MZ_ZIP_FLAG_CASE_SENSITIVE: u32 = 0x0100;
pub const MZ_ZIP_FLAG_IGNORE_PATH: u32 = 0x0800;
pub const MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA: u32 = 0x20000;
pub const MZ_ZIP_FLAG_DO_NOT_SORT_CENTRAL_DIRECTORY: u32 = 0x40000;

// --- Module: mz_p4 ---
// Constants from the original C code
const TINFL_LZ_DICT_SIZE: usize = 32768;

// Flags

// Error types
const MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER: i32 = -1;
const MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_ENCRYPTION: i32 = -2;
const MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_METHOD: i32 = -3;
const MZ_ZIP_FILE_READ_FAILED: i32 = -4;
const MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED: i32 = -5;
const MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED: i32 = -6;
const MZ_ZIP_WRITE_CALLBACK_FAILED: i32 = -7;
const MZ_ZIP_DECOMPRESSION_FAILED: i32 = -8;
const MZ_ZIP_UNEXPECTED_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE: i32 = -9;
const MZ_ZIP_INTERNAL_ERROR: i32 = -10;
const MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE: i32 = -11;
const MZ_ZIP_FILE_OPEN_FAILED: i32 = -12;
const MZ_ZIP_FILE_CLOSE_FAILED: i32 = -13;

// Decompression status
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]

// Bit flags

// Compression methods

// We'll assume these types exist from previous translations
type MzUint = u32;
type MzUint64 = u64;
type MzBool = bool;
type MzFile = *mut std::ffi::c_void;

struct MzZipArchive {
    m_pState: Option<Box<MzZipInternalState>>,
    m_pRead: fn(*mut std::ffi::c_void, MzUint64, &mut [u8]) -> usize,
    m_pAlloc: fn(*mut std::ffi::c_void, usize, usize) -> *mut std::ffi::c_void,
    m_pFree: fn(*mut std::ffi::c_void, *mut std::ffi::c_void),
    m_pIO_opaque: *mut std::ffi::c_void,
    m_archive_size: MzUint64,
    m_pAlloc_opaque: *mut std::ffi::c_void,
}

struct MzZipInternalState {
    m_pMem: Option<Vec<u8>>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
struct MzZipArchiveFileStat {
    m_is_directory: bool,
    m_is_supported: bool,
    m_method: u16,
    m_comp_size: MzUint64,
    m_uncomp_size: MzUint64,
    m_local_header_ofs: MzUint64,
    m_bit_flag: u16,
    m_crc32: u32,
    m_time: u32,
}

struct MzZipReaderExtractIterState {
    pZip: *mut MzZipArchive,
    file_stat: MzZipArchiveFileStat,
    flags: MzUint,
    status: TinflStatus,
    file_crc32: u32,
    read_buf_ofs: usize,
    out_buf_ofs: MzUint64,
    pRead_buf: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    pWrite_buf: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    out_blk_remain: usize,
    cur_file_ofs: MzUint64,
    read_buf_size: MzUint64,
    read_buf_avail: MzUint64,
    comp_remaining: MzUint64,
    inflator: Option<tinfl::DecompressorOxide>,
}

type MzFileWriteFunc = fn(*mut std::ffi::c_void, MzUint64, &[u8]) -> usize;

    // This would be implemented elsewhere - returning a placeholder
    true
}
