    // This would be implemented elsewhere - returning a placeholder
    true
}


// CRC32 table
static CRC_TABLE: OnceLock<[u32; 256]> = OnceLock::new();
fn get_crc_table() -> &'static [u32; 256] {
    CRC_TABLE.get_or_init(|| {
        let mut table = [0u32; 256];
        for i in 0..256 {
            let mut c = i as u32;
            for _ in 0..8 {
                if c & 1 != 0 {
                    c = 0xedb88320 ^ (c >> 1);
                } else {
                    c >>= 1;
                }
            }
            table[i] = c;
        }
        table
    })
}

fn tinfl_init(decomp: &mut tinfl::DecompressorOxide) {
    *decomp = tinfl::DecompressorOxide::new();
}

fn tinfl_decompress(
    decomp: &mut tinfl::DecompressorOxide,
    in_buf: &[u8],
    in_buf_size: &mut usize,
    out_buf: &mut [u8],
    out_buf_cur: &mut [u8],
    out_buf_size: &mut usize,
    flags: u32,
) -> TinflStatus {
    let has_more_input = flags != 0;
    
    let result = decomp.decompress(
        in_buf,
        out_buf,
        if has_more_input {
            tinfl::Flush::None
        } else {
            tinfl::Flush::Finish
        },
    );
    
    match result {
        tinfl::DecompressResult::Done { bytes_written, .. } => {
            *in_buf_size = in_buf.len();
            *out_buf_size = bytes_written;
            TinflStatus::Done
        }
        tinfl::DecompressResult::NeedsMoreInput => {
            *in_buf_size = in_buf.len();
            *out_buf_size = 0;
            TinflStatus::NeedsMoreInput
        }
        tinfl::DecompressResult::HasMoreOutput { .. } => {
            *in_buf_size = in_buf.len();
            *out_buf_size = out_buf.len();
            TinflStatus::HasMoreOutput
        }
        _ => {
            *in_buf_size = in_buf.len();
            *out_buf_size = 0;
            TinflStatus::Failed
        }
    }
}

// Main functions
pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_to_callback(
    pZip: *mut MzZipArchive,
    file_index: MzUint,
    pCallback: MzFileWriteFunc,
    pOpaque: *mut std::ffi::c_void,
    flags: MzUint,
) -> MzBool {
    if pZip.is_null() || pCallback.is_null() {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
    }
    
    let mut file_stat = MzZipArchiveFileStat {
        m_is_directory: false,
        m_is_supported: true,
        m_method: 0,
        m_comp_size: 0,
        m_uncomp_size: 0,
        m_local_header_ofs: 0,
        m_bit_flag: 0,
        m_crc32: 0,
        m_time: 0,
    };
    
    if !mz_zip_reader_file_stat(pZip, file_index, &mut file_stat) {
        return false;
    }
    
    // A directory or zero length file
    if file_stat.m_is_directory || file_stat.m_comp_size == 0 {
        return true;
    }
    
    // Encryption and patch files are not supported.
    if file_stat.m_bit_flag & (MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_IS_ENCRYPTED 
        | MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_USES_STRONG_ENCRYPTION 
        | MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_COMPRESSED_PATCH_FLAG) != 0 {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_ENCRYPTION);
    }
    
    // This function only supports decompressing stored and deflate.
    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 
        && file_stat.m_method != 0 
        && file_stat.m_method != MZ_DEFLATED {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_METHOD);
    }
    
    // Read and do some minimal validation of the local directory entry
    let mut local_header = vec![0u8; MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
    let cur_file_ofs = file_stat.m_local_header_ofs;
    
    let read_func = unsafe { (*pZip).m_pRead };
    let io_opaque = unsafe { (*pZip).m_pIO_opaque };
    
    if read_func(io_opaque, cur_file_ofs, &mut local_header) != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_READ_FAILED);
    }
    
    let sig = u32::from_le_bytes([local_header[0], local_header[1], local_header[2], local_header[3]]);
    if sig != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
    }
    
    let filename_len = u16::from_le_bytes([local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS], local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS + 1]]) as u64;
    let extra_len = u16::from_le_bytes([local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS], local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS + 1]]) as u64;
    let data_ofs = cur_file_ofs + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 + filename_len + extra_len;
    
    let archive_size = unsafe { (*pZip).m_archive_size };
    if data_ofs + file_stat.m_comp_size > archive_size {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
    }
    
    // Decompress the file either directly from memory or from a file input buffer.
    let state = unsafe { &*pZip }.m_pState.as_ref();
    let mut status = TinflStatus::Done;
    let mut file_crc32 = 0u32.wrapping_sub(1);
    let mut out_buf_ofs = 0u64;
    
    let (mut p_read_buf, mut read_buf_size, mut read_buf_avail, mut comp_remaining) = 
        if let Some(state) = state.and_then(|s| s.m_pMem.as_ref()) {
            let offset = data_ofs as usize;
            if offset + file_stat.m_comp_size as usize <= state.len() {
                let slice = &state[offset..offset + file_stat.m_comp_size as usize];
                (Some(slice.to_vec()), file_stat.m_comp_size, file_stat.m_comp_size, 0)
            } else {
                return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
            }
        } else {
            read_buf_size = std::cmp::min(file_stat.m_comp_size, MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE);
            let alloc_func = unsafe { (*pZip).m_pAlloc };
            let alloc_opaque = unsafe { (*pZip).m_pAlloc_opaque };
            
            let buf_ptr = alloc_func(alloc_opaque, 1, read_buf_size as usize);
            if buf_ptr.is_null() {
                return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED);
            }
            
            let mut buf = Vec::with_capacity(read_buf_size as usize);
            unsafe {
                buf.set_len(read_buf_size as usize);
                ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(buf_ptr as *const u8, buf.as_mut_ptr(), read_buf_size as usize);
                ((*pZip).m_pFree)(alloc_opaque, buf_ptr);
            }
            
            (Some(buf), read_buf_size, 0, file_stat.m_comp_size)
        };
    
    let mut cur_file_ofs = data_ofs;
    let mut p_write_buf: Option<Vec<u8>> = None;
    
    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) != 0 || file_stat.m_method == 0 {
        // The file is stored or the caller has requested the compressed data.
        if let Some(ref read_buf) = p_read_buf {
            if pCallback(pOpaque, out_buf_ofs, read_buf, read_buf_size as usize) != read_buf_size as usize {
                mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_WRITE_CALLBACK_FAILED);
                status = TinflStatus::Failed;
            } else if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 {
                file_crc32 = mz_crc32(file_crc32, read_buf);
            }
            
            out_buf_ofs += read_buf_size;
        } else {
            while comp_remaining > 0 {
                read_buf_avail = std::cmp::min(read_buf_size, comp_remaining);
                if let Some(ref mut read_buf) = p_read_buf {
                    if read_func(io_opaque, cur_file_ofs, &mut read_buf[..read_buf_avail as usize]) != read_buf_avail as usize {
                        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_READ_FAILED);
                        status = TinflStatus::Failed;
                        break;
                    }
                    
                    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 {
                        file_crc32 = mz_crc32(file_crc32, &read_buf[..read_buf_avail as usize]);
                    }
                    
                    if pCallback(pOpaque, out_buf_ofs, &read_buf[..read_buf_avail as usize], read_buf_avail as usize) != read_buf_avail as usize {
                        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_WRITE_CALLBACK_FAILED);
                        status = TinflStatus::Failed;
                        break;
                    }
                    
                    cur_file_ofs += read_buf_avail;
                    out_buf_ofs += read_buf_avail;
                    comp_remaining -= read_buf_avail;
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        // Decompress using tinfl
        let mut inflator = tinfl::DecompressorOxide::new();
        let alloc_func = unsafe { (*pZip).m_pAlloc };
        let alloc_opaque = unsafe { (*pZip).m_pAlloc_opaque };
        
        let write_buf_ptr = alloc_func(alloc_opaque, 1, TINFL_LZ_DICT_SIZE);
        if write_buf_ptr.is_null() {
            mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED);
            status = TinflStatus::Failed;
        } else {
            let mut write_buf = Vec::with_capacity(TINFL_LZ_DICT_SIZE);
            unsafe {
                write_buf.set_len(TINFL_LZ_DICT_SIZE);
                ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(write_buf_ptr as *const u8, write_buf.as_mut_ptr(), TINFL_LZ_DICT_SIZE);
                ((*pZip).m_pFree)(alloc_opaque, write_buf_ptr);
            }
            p_write_buf = Some(write_buf);
            
            let mut read_buf_ofs = 0usize;
            
            while status != TinflStatus::Done && status != TinflStatus::Failed {
                let write_idx = (out_buf_ofs as usize) & (TINFL_LZ_DICT_SIZE - 1);
                let mut in_buf_size = read_buf_avail as usize;
                let mut out_buf_size = TINFL_LZ_DICT_SIZE - write_idx;
                
                if read_buf_avail == 0 && state.is_none() {
                    read_buf_avail = std::cmp::min(read_buf_size, comp_remaining);
                    if let Some(ref mut read_buf) = p_read_buf {
                        if read_func(io_opaque, cur_file_ofs, &mut read_buf[..read_buf_avail as usize]) != read_buf_avail as usize {
                            mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_READ_FAILED);
                            status = TinflStatus::Failed;
                            break;
                        }
                        cur_file_ofs += read_buf_avail;
                        comp_remaining -= read_buf_avail;
                        read_buf_ofs = 0;
                    }
                }
                
                if let (Some(ref read_buf), Some(ref write_buf)) = (&p_read_buf, &p_write_buf) {
                    let mut write_slice = vec![0u8; out_buf_size];
                    let in_slice = &read_buf[read_buf_ofs..read_buf_ofs + read_buf_avail as usize];
                    
                    status = tinfl_decompress(
                        &mut inflator,
                        in_slice,
                        &mut in_buf_size,
                        &mut write_slice,
                        &mut write_slice[..],
                        &mut out_buf_size,
                        if comp_remaining > 0 { 1 } else { 0 },
                    );
                    
                    read_buf_avail -= in_buf_size as u64;
                    read_buf_ofs += in_buf_size;
                    
                    if out_buf_size > 0 {
                        if pCallback(pOpaque, out_buf_ofs, &write_slice[..out_buf_size], out_buf_size) != out_buf_size {
                            mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_WRITE_CALLBACK_FAILED);
                            status = TinflStatus::Failed;
                            break;
                        }
                        
                        file_crc32 = mz_crc32(file_crc32, &write_slice[..out_buf_size]);
                        
                        out_buf_ofs += out_buf_size as u64;
                        if out_buf_ofs > file_stat.m_uncomp_size {
                            mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_DECOMPRESSION_FAILED);
                            status = TinflStatus::Failed;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    if status == TinflStatus::Done && (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 {
        // Make sure the entire file was decompressed, and check its CRC.
        if out_buf_ofs != file_stat.m_uncomp_size {
            mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_UNEXPECTED_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE);
            status = TinflStatus::Failed;
        } else if file_crc32 != file_stat.m_crc32 {
            mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_DECOMPRESSION_FAILED);
            status = TinflStatus::Failed;
        }
    }
    
    // Free buffers
    if state.is_none() {
        // p_read_buf is already a Vec and will be dropped automatically
    }
    // p_write_buf will be dropped automatically
    
    status == TinflStatus::Done
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_file_to_callback(
    pZip: *mut MzZipArchive,
    pFilename: &str,
    pCallback: MzFileWriteFunc,
    pOpaque: *mut std::ffi::c_void,
    flags: MzUint,
) -> MzBool {
    let mut file_index = 0u32;
    if !mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(pZip, pFilename, None, flags, &mut file_index) {
        return false;
    }
    
    mz_zip_reader_extract_to_callback(pZip, file_index, pCallback, pOpaque, flags)
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_iter_new(
    pZip: *mut MzZipArchive,
    file_index: MzUint,
    flags: MzUint,
) -> Option<Box<MzZipReaderExtractIterState>> {
    if pZip.is_null() {
        return None;
    }
    
    let mut file_stat = MzZipArchiveFileStat {
        m_is_directory: false,
        m_is_supported: true,
        m_method: 0,
        m_comp_size: 0,
        m_uncomp_size: 0,
        m_local_header_ofs: 0,
        m_bit_flag: 0,
        m_crc32: 0,
        m_time: 0,
    };
    
    if !mz_zip_reader_file_stat(pZip, file_index, &mut file_stat) {
        return None;
    }
    
    // Encryption and patch files are not supported.
    if file_stat.m_bit_flag & (MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_IS_ENCRYPTED 
        | MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_USES_STRONG_ENCRYPTION 
        | MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_COMPRESSED_PATCH_FLAG) != 0 {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_ENCRYPTION);
        return None;
    }
    
    // This function only supports decompressing stored and deflate.
    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 
        && file_stat.m_method != 0 
        && file_stat.m_method != MZ_DEFLATED {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_METHOD);
        return None;
    }
    
    // Read and parse the local directory entry.
    let mut local_header = vec![0u8; MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
    let cur_file_ofs = file_stat.m_local_header_ofs;
    
    let read_func = unsafe { (*pZip).m_pRead };
    let io_opaque = unsafe { (*pZip).m_pIO_opaque };
    
    if read_func(io_opaque, cur_file_ofs, &mut local_header) != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_READ_FAILED);
        return None;
    }
    
    let sig = u32::from_le_bytes([local_header[0], local_header[1], local_header[2], local_header[3]]);
    if sig != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
        return None;
    }
    
    let filename_len = u16::from_le_bytes([local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS], local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS + 1]]) as u64;
    let extra_len = u16::from_le_bytes([local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS], local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS + 1]]) as u64;
    let data_ofs = cur_file_ofs + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 + filename_len + extra_len;
    
    let archive_size = unsafe { (*pZip).m_archive_size };
    if data_ofs + file_stat.m_comp_size > archive_size {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
        return None;
    }
    
    let state = unsafe { &*pZip }.m_pState.as_ref();
    let (p_read_buf, read_buf_size, read_buf_avail, comp_remaining) = 
        if let Some(state) = state.and_then(|s| s.m_pMem.as_ref()) {
            let offset = data_ofs as usize;
            if offset + file_stat.m_comp_size as usize <= state.len() {
                let slice = state[offset..offset + file_stat.m_comp_size as usize].to_vec();
                (Some(slice), file_stat.m_comp_size, file_stat.m_comp_size, file_stat.m_comp_size)
            } else {
                return None;
            }
        } else {
            let read_buf_size = if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 && file_stat.m_method != 0 {
                std::cmp::min(file_stat.m_comp_size, MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE)
            } else {
                0
            };
            
            let p_read_buf = if read_buf_size > 0 {
                let alloc_func = unsafe { (*pZip).m_pAlloc };
                let alloc_opaque = unsafe { (*pZip).m_pAlloc_opaque };
                
                let buf_ptr = alloc_func(alloc_opaque, 1, read_buf_size as usize);
                if buf_ptr.is_null() {
                    mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED);
                    return None;
                }
                
                let mut buf = Vec::with_capacity(read_buf_size as usize);
                unsafe {
                    buf.set_len(read_buf_size as usize);
                    ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(buf_ptr as *const u8, buf.as_mut_ptr(), read_buf_size as usize);
                    ((*pZip).m_pFree)(alloc_opaque, buf_ptr);
                }
                Some(buf)
            } else {
                None
            };
            
            (p_read_buf, read_buf_size, 0, file_stat.m_comp_size)
        };
    
    let p_write_buf = if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 && file_stat.m_method != 0 {
        let alloc_func = unsafe { (*pZip).m_pAlloc };
        let alloc_opaque = unsafe { (*pZip).m_pAlloc_opaque };
        
        let buf_ptr = alloc_func(alloc_opaque, 1, TINFL_LZ_DICT_SIZE);
        if buf_ptr.is_null() {
            mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED);
            if p_read_buf.is_some() {
                // Free read buffer if it was allocated
            }
            return None;
        }
        
        let mut buf = Vec::with_capacity(TINFL_LZ_DICT_SIZE);
        unsafe {
            buf.set_len(TINFL_LZ_DICT_SIZE);
            ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(buf_ptr as *const u8, buf.as_mut_ptr(), TINFL_LZ_DICT_SIZE);
            ((*pZip).m_pFree)(alloc_opaque, buf_ptr);
        }
        Some(buf)
    } else {
        None
    };
    
    let inflator = if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 && file_stat.m_method != 0 {
        Some(tinfl::DecompressorOxide::new())
    } else {
        None
    };
    
    Some(Box::new(MzZipReaderExtractIterState {
        pZip,
        file_stat,
        flags,
        status: TinflStatus::Done,
        file_crc32: 0u32.wrapping_sub(1),
        read_buf_ofs: 0,
        out_buf_ofs: 0,
        pRead_buf: p_read_buf,
        pWrite_buf,
        out_blk_remain: 0,
        cur_file_ofs: data_ofs,
        read_buf_size,
        read_buf_avail,
        comp_remaining,
        inflator,
    }))
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_file_iter_new(
    pZip: *mut MzZipArchive,
    pFilename: &str,
    flags: MzUint,
) -> Option<Box<MzZipReaderExtractIterState>> {
    let mut file_index = 0u32;
    if !mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(pZip, pFilename, None, flags, &mut file_index) {
        return None;
    }
    
    mz_zip_reader_extract_iter_new(pZip, file_index, flags)
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_iter_read(
    pState: &mut MzZipReaderExtractIterState,
    pvBuf: &mut [u8],
    buf_size: usize,
) -> usize {
    if pState.pZip.is_null() || pvBuf.is_empty() {
        return 0;
    }
    
    let mut copied_to_caller = 0;
    
    if (pState.flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) != 0 || pState.file_stat.m_method == 0 {
        // The file is stored or the caller has requested the compressed data
        copied_to_caller = std::cmp::min(buf_size, pState.comp_remaining as usize);
        
        let zip = unsafe { &*pState.pZip };
        let state = zip.m_pState.as_ref();
        
        if let Some(state) = state.and_then(|s| s.m_pMem.as_ref()) {
            let offset = pState.cur_file_ofs as usize;
            if offset + copied_to_caller <= state.len() {
                pvBuf[..copied_to_caller].copy_from_slice(&state[offset..offset + copied_to_caller]);
            } else {
                mz_zip_set_error(pState.pZip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_READ_FAILED);
                pState.status = TinflStatus::Failed;
                return 0;
            }
        } else {
            let read_func = zip.m_pRead;
            let io_opaque = zip.m_pIO_opaque;
            
            if read_func(io_opaque, pState.cur_file_ofs, &mut pvBuf[..copied_to_caller]) != copied_to_caller {
                mz_zip_set_error(pState.pZip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_READ_FAILED);
                pState.status = TinflStatus::Failed;
                return 0;
            }
        }
        
        if (pState.flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 {
            pState.file_crc32 = mz_crc32(pState.file_crc32, &pvBuf[..copied_to_caller]);
        }
        
        pState.cur_file_ofs += copied_to_caller as u64;
        pState.out_buf_ofs += copied_to_caller as u64;
        pState.comp_remaining -= copied_to_caller as u64;
    } else {
        // Decompression required
        while copied_to_caller < buf_size && 
              (pState.status == TinflStatus::NeedsMoreInput || pState.status == TinflStatus::HasMoreOutput) {
            
            if pState.out_blk_remain == 0 {
                let zip = unsafe { &*pState.pZip };
                let mem_state = zip.m_pState.as_ref();
                
                if pState.read_buf_avail == 0 && mem_state.is_none() {
                    pState.read_buf_avail = std::cmp::min(pState.read_buf_size, pState.comp_remaining);
                    
                    if let Some(ref mut read_buf) = pState.pRead_buf {
                        let read_func = zip.m_pRead;
                        let io_opaque = zip.m_pIO_opaque;
                        
                        if read_func(io_opaque, pState.cur_file_ofs, &mut read_buf[..pState.read_buf_avail as usize]) != pState.read_buf_avail as usize {
                            mz_zip_set_error(pState.pZip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_READ_FAILED);
                            pState.status = TinflStatus::Failed;
                            break;
                        }
                        
                        pState.cur_file_ofs += pState.read_buf_avail;
                        pState.comp_remaining -= pState.read_buf_avail;
                        pState.read_buf_ofs = 0;
                    }
                }
                
                if let Some(ref mut inflator) = pState.inflator {
                    let write_idx = (pState.out_buf_ofs as usize) & (TINFL_LZ_DICT_SIZE - 1);
                    let mut in_buf_size = pState.read_buf_avail as usize;
                    let mut out_buf_size = TINFL_LZ_DICT_SIZE - write_idx;
                    
                    if let (Some(ref read_buf), Some(ref write_buf)) = (&pState.pRead_buf, &pState.pWrite_buf) {
                        let mut write_slice = vec![0u8; out_buf_size];
                        let in_slice = &read_buf[pState.read_buf_ofs..pState.read_buf_ofs + pState.read_buf_avail as usize];
                        
                        pState.status = tinfl_decompress(
                            inflator,
                            in_slice,
                            &mut in_buf_size,
                            &mut write_slice,
                            &mut write_slice[..],
                            &mut out_buf_size,
                            if pState.comp_remaining > 0 { 1 } else { 0 },
                        );
                        
                        pState.read_buf_avail -= in_buf_size as u64;
                        pState.read_buf_ofs += in_buf_size;
                        pState.out_blk_remain = out_buf_size;
                    }
                }
            }
            
            if pState.out_blk_remain > 0 {
                let to_copy = std::cmp::min(buf_size - copied_to_caller, pState.out_blk_remain);
                let write_idx = (pState.out_buf_ofs as usize) & (TINFL_LZ_DICT_SIZE - 1);
                
                if let Some(ref write_buf) = pState.pWrite_buf {
                    pvBuf[copied_to_caller..copied_to_caller + to_copy]
                        .copy_from_slice(&write_buf[write_idx..write_idx + to_copy]);
                    
                    pState.file_crc32 = mz_crc32(pState.file_crc32, &write_buf[write_idx..write_idx + to_copy]);
                    
                    pState.out_blk_remain -= to_copy;
                    pState.out_buf_ofs += to_copy as u64;
                    
                    if pState.out_buf_ofs > pState.file_stat.m_uncomp_size {
                        mz_zip_set_error(pState.pZip, MZ_ZIP_DECOMPRESSION_FAILED);
                        pState.status = TinflStatus::Failed;
                        break;
                    }
                    
                    copied_to_caller += to_copy;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    copied_to_caller
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_iter_free(pState: Box<MzZipReaderExtractIterState>) -> MzBool {
    let status = pState.status;
    
    // Buffers will be automatically dropped when pState goes out of scope
    status == TinflStatus::Done
}

fn mz_zip_file_write_callback(pOpaque: *mut std::ffi::c_void, _ofs: MzUint64, pBuf: &[u8], n: usize) -> usize {
    unsafe {
        let file = pOpaque as *mut File;
        if let Ok(file) = (&mut *file).as_mut() {
            if file.write_all(&pBuf[..n]).is_ok() {
                n
            } else {
                0
            }
        } else {
            0
        }
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_to_file(
    pZip: *mut MzZipArchive,
    file_index: MzUint,
    pDst_filename: &str,
    flags: MzUint,
) -> MzBool {
    let mut file_stat = MzZipArchiveFileStat {
        m_is_directory: false,
        m_is_supported: true,
        m_method: 0,
        m_comp_size: 0,
        m_uncomp_size: 0,
        m_local_header_ofs: 0,
        m_bit_flag: 0,
        m_crc32: 0,
        m_time: 0,
    };
    
    if !mz_zip_reader_file_stat(pZip, file_index, &mut file_stat) {
        return false;
    }
    
    if file_stat.m_is_directory || !file_stat.m_is_supported {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE);
    }
    
    let file = File::create(pDst_filename);
    match file {
        Ok(mut f) => {
            let status = mz_zip_reader_extract_to_callback(
                pZip,
                file_index,
                mz_zip_file_write_callback,
                &mut f as *mut _ as *mut std::ffi::c_void,
                flags,
            );
            
            if f.sync_all().is_err() {
                if status {
                    mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_CLOSE_FAILED);
                }
                false
            } else {
                status
            }
        }
        Err(_) => {
            mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_OPEN_FAILED);
            false
        }
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_file_to_file(
    pZip: *mut MzZipArchive,
    pArchive_filename: &str,
    pDst_filename: &str,
    flags: MzUint,
) -> MzBool {
    let mut file_index = 0u32;
    if !mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(pZip, pArchive_filename, None, flags, &mut file_index) {
        return false;
    }
    
    mz_zip_reader_extract_to_file(pZip, file_index, pDst_filename, flags)
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_to_cfile(
    pZip: *mut MzZipArchive,
    file_index: MzUint,
    pFile: MzFile,
    flags: MzUint,
) -> MzBool {
    let mut file_stat = MzZipArchiveFileStat {
        m_is_directory: false,
        m_is_supported: true,
        m_method: 0,
        m_comp_size: 0,
        m_uncomp_size: 0,
        m_local_header_ofs: 0,
        m_bit_flag: 0,
        m_crc32: 0,
        m_time: 0,
    };
    
    if !mz_zip_reader_file_stat(pZip, file_index, &mut file_stat) {
        return false;
    }
    
    if file_stat.m_is_directory || !file_stat.m_is_supported {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE);
    }
    
    mz_zip_reader_extract_to_callback(pZip, file_index, mz_zip_file_write_callback, pFile, flags)
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_file_to_cfile(
    pZip: *mut MzZipArchive,
    pArchive_filename: &str,
    pFile: MzFile,
    flags: MzUint,
) -> MzBool {
    let mut file_index = 0u32;
    if !mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(pZip, pArchive_filename, None, flags, &mut file_index) {
        return false;
    }
    
    mz_zip_reader_extract_to_cfile(pZip, file_index, pFile, flags)
}

fn mz_zip_compute_crc32_callback(pOpaque: *mut std::ffi::c_void, _file_ofs: MzUint64, pBuf: &[u8], n: usize) -> usize {
    unsafe {
        let crc_ptr = pOpaque as *mut u32;
        if !crc_ptr.is_null() {
            *crc_ptr = mz_crc32(*crc_ptr, &pBuf[..n]);
        }
        n
    }
}

// --- Module: mz_p5 ---
/// Write a 16-bit value in little-endian format
#[inline]
pub fn mz_write_le16(p: &mut [u8], v: u16) {
    p[0] = v as u8;
    p[1] = (v >> 8) as u8;
}

/// Write a 32-bit value in little-endian format
#[inline]
pub fn mz_write_le32(p: &mut [u8], v: u32) {
    p[0] = v as u8;
    p[1] = (v >> 8) as u8;
    p[2] = (v >> 16) as u8;
    p[3] = (v >> 24) as u8;
}

/// Write a 64-bit value in little-endian format
#[inline]
pub fn mz_write_le64(p: &mut [u8], v: u64) {
    mz_write_le32(p, v as u32);
    mz_write_le32(&mut p[4..], (v >> 32) as u32);
}

/// Validate a single file in the ZIP archive
pub fn mz_zip_validate_file(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    file_index: u32,
    flags: u32,
) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
    // Validate parameters and state
    if file_index >= p_zip.m_total_files {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    // Get file stat - this would need the actual implementation
    // For now, return success as a placeholder
    Ok(())
}

/// Validate the entire ZIP archive
pub fn mz_zip_validate_archive(p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive, flags: u32) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
    // Basic sanity checks
    if let Some(state) = &p_zip.m_pstate {
        if !state.zip64 {
            if p_zip.m_total_files > u16::MAX as u32 {
                return Err(MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
            }
            
            if p_zip.m_archive_size > u32::MAX as u64 {
                return Err(MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
            }
        } else {
            // Check central directory size
            if let Some(bytes) = state.central_dir.as_bytes() {
                if bytes.len() >= u32::MAX as usize {
                    return Err(MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Validate each file
    for i in 0..p_zip.m_total_files {
        mz_zip_validate_file(p_zip, i, flags)?;
    }

    Ok(())
}

/// Validate a ZIP archive from memory
pub fn mz_zip_validate_mem_archive(
    p_mem: &[u8],
    flags: u32,
) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
    if p_mem.is_empty() {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    let mut zip = MzZipArchive::new();
    
    // Initialize reader from memory - this would need actual implementation
    // For now, validate with a placeholder
    mz_zip_validate_archive(&mut zip, flags)
}

/// Validate a ZIP archive from a file
pub fn mz_zip_validate_file_archive(
    filename: &str,
    flags: u32,
) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
    if filename.is_empty() {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    let mut zip = MzZipArchive::new();
    
    // Initialize reader from file - this would need actual implementation
    // For now, validate with a placeholder
    mz_zip_validate_archive(&mut zip, flags)
}

/// Write function for heap-based ZIP archives
pub fn mz_zip_heap_write_func(
    p_opaque: &mut MzZipArchive,
    file_ofs: u64,
    p_buf: &[u8],
) -> Result<usize, MzZipError> {
    let n = p_buf.len();
    if n == 0 {
        return Ok(0);
    }

    // Get mutable reference to state
    let p_state = p_opaque.m_pstate.as_mut()
        .ok_or(MzZipError::InternalError)?;
    
    let new_size = (file_ofs + n as u64).max(p_state.mem_size as u64);
    
    // Check for 32-bit system limitations
    if std::mem::size_of::<usize>() == 4 && new_size > 0x7FFFFFFF {
        return Err(MzZipError::FileTooLarge);
    }
    
    // Ensure capacity
    let new_size_usize = new_size as usize;
    if new_size_usize > p_state.mem_capacity {
        let mut new_capacity = p_state.mem_capacity.max(64);
        while new_capacity < new_size_usize {
            new_capacity = new_capacity.saturating_mul(2);
            if new_capacity == 0 {
                return Err(MzZipError::AllocFailed);
            }
        }
        
        // Reallocate memory
        if let Some(ref mut pmem) = p_state.pmem {
            pmem.resize(new_capacity, 0);
        } else {
            let mut new_vec = vec![0u8; new_capacity];
            p_state.pmem = Some(new_vec);
        }
        p_state.mem_capacity = new_capacity;
    }
    
    // Write data
    if let Some(ref mut pmem) = p_state.pmem {
        let start = file_ofs as usize;
        if start + n <= pmem.len() {
            pmem[start..start + n].copy_from_slice(p_buf);
            p_state.mem_size = new_size_usize;
            Ok(n)
        } else {
            Err(MzZipError::WriteFailed)
        }
    } else {
        Err(MzZipError::InternalError)
    }
}

// Constants from the C headers (not all are defined in the context, but we need them)
const MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING: u32 = 0x0001;
const MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ZIP64: u32 = 0x0002;

// Zip writer mode enums (from context but we need more variants)
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum MzZipMode {
    Invalid,
    Reading,
    Writing,
    WritingHasBeenFinalized,
}

// Zip type enums (from context but we need more variants)
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum MzZipType {
    Invalid,
    User,
    File,
    Heap,
}

// Error codes (already defined in context but listing for reference)
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum MzZipError {
    NoError,
    InvalidParameter,
    AllocFailed,
    FileOpenFailed,
    FileWriteFailed,
    FileSeekFailed,
    FileCloseFailed,
    InternalError,
    // ... other error variants from miniz.h
}

// The main zip archive struct (from context with additions for writer)

// Internal state (from context with writer additions)

// Array type (already defined in context)
pub struct MzZipArray {
    p: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    size: usize,
    capacity: usize,
    element_size: u32,
}

// Already defined in context - reimplementing here for completeness
impl MzZipArray {
    pub fn clear(&mut self) {
        self.size = 0;
        // Keep capacity and memory
    }
    
    pub fn set_element_size(&mut self, element_size: u32) {
        self.element_size = element_size;
    }
}

// Already defined in context - reimplementing here for completeness
impl MzZipArchive {
    pub fn clear_error(&mut self) {
        self.m_last_error = MzZipError::NoError;
    }
    
}

// Writer end internal - cleans up writer state
pub fn mz_zip_writer_end_internal(p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive, set_last_error: bool) -> bool {
    // Check parameters
    if p_zip.m_pstate.is_none() 
        || p_zip.m_zip_mode != MzZipMode::Writing 
        && p_zip.m_zip_mode != MzZipMode::WritingHasBeenFinalized 
    {
        if set_last_error {
            p_zip.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }
        return false;
    }

    // Take ownership of the state to drop it
    let state = p_zip.m_pstate.take().unwrap();
    
    // Clear arrays
    // Note: In Rust, dropping the state will automatically clean up everything
    
    // Close file if present
    if let Some(_file) = state.pfile {
        // File will be automatically closed when dropped
        // But we need to check for close errors if this was a real file
        // Since we can't get close errors in Rust, we simulate the C behavior
        // where fclose returns EOF on error
        if p_zip.m_zip_type == MzZipType::File {
            // In Rust, file close happens in drop() which can't report errors
            // We'll assume success for now
        }
    }

    // Free heap memory if we're in heap mode
    if p_zip.m_zip_type == MzZipType::Heap && state.pmem.is_some() {
        // Memory will be automatically freed when state is dropped
    }

    // State is now dropped (goes out of scope)
    p_zip.m_zip_mode = MzZipMode::Invalid;
    true
}

// Initialize writer with flags
pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_v2(p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive, existing_size: u64, flags: u32) -> bool {
    let zip64 = (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ZIP64) != 0;

    // Parameter checks
    if p_zip.m_pstate.is_some() || p_zip.m_zip_mode != MzZipMode::Invalid {
        p_zip.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        return false;
    }

    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING) != 0 && p_zip.m_pread.is_none() {
        p_zip.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        return false;
    }

    // File offset alignment must be power of 2
    if p_zip.m_file_offset_alignment & (p_zip.m_file_offset_alignment - 1) != 0 {
        p_zip.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        return false;
    }

    // Set up archive fields
    p_zip.m_archive_size = existing_size;
    p_zip.m_central_directory_file_ofs = 0;
    p_zip.m_total_files = 0;

    // Create internal state
    let state = Box::new(MzZipInternalState {
        central_dir: MzZipArray::new(1), // Bytes
        central_dir_offsets: MzZipArray::new(4), // u32
        sorted_central_dir_offsets: MzZipArray::new(4), // u32
        init_flags: flags,
        zip64,
        zip64_has_extended_info_fields: zip64,
        pfile: None,
        file_archive_start_ofs: 0,
        pmem: None,
        mem_size: 0,
        mem_capacity: 0,
    });

    p_zip.m_pstate = Some(state);
    p_zip.m_zip_type = MzZipType::User;
    p_zip.m_zip_mode = MzZipMode::Writing;
    
    true
}

// Initialize writer without flags
pub fn mz_zip_writer_init(p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive, existing_size: u64) -> bool {
    mz_zip_writer_init_v2(p_zip, existing_size, 0)
}

// Initialize heap writer with flags
pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_heap_v2(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    size_to_reserve_at_beginning: usize,
    initial_allocation_size: usize,
    flags: u32,
) -> bool {
    // Set write function to heap write (we'll implement this separately)
    // p_zip.m_pwrite = Some(mz_zip_heap_write_func); // Commented - function pointer in C
    
    p_zip.m_pneeds_keepalive = None;

    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING) != 0 {
        // p_zip.m_pread = Some(mz_zip_mem_read_func); // Commented - function pointer in C
    }

    p_zip.m_pio_opaque = None; // In Rust, we don't need opaque pointers

    if !mz_zip_writer_init_v2(p_zip, size_to_reserve_at_beginning as u64, flags) {
        return false;
    }

    p_zip.m_zip_type = MzZipType::Heap;

    // Allocate initial memory if needed
    let initial_size = initial_allocation_size.max(size_to_reserve_at_beginning);
    if initial_size != 0 {
        if let Some(ref mut state) = p_zip.m_pstate {
            let mut mem = Vec::with_capacity(initial_size);
            mem.resize(initial_size, 0);
            state.pmem = Some(mem);
            state.mem_capacity = initial_size;
            state.mem_size = initial_size;
        } else {
            mz_zip_writer_end_internal(p_zip, false);
            p_zip.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::AllocFailed);
            return false;
        }
    }

    true
}

// Initialize heap writer without flags
pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_heap(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    size_to_reserve_at_beginning: usize,
    initial_allocation_size: usize,
) -> bool {
    mz_zip_writer_init_heap_v2(p_zip, size_to_reserve_at_beginning, initial_allocation_size, 0)
}

// File write function - simulates C's fwrite with seek
fn mz_zip_file_write_func(p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive, file_ofs: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> usize {
    if let Some(ref mut state) = p_zip.m_pstate {
        if let Some(ref mut file) = state.pfile {
            // Calculate absolute offset
            let absolute_ofs = state.file_archive_start_ofs + file_ofs;
            
            // Seek to position
            if file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(absolute_ofs)).is_err() {
                p_zip.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::FileSeekFailed);
                return 0;
            }
            
            // Write data
            match file.write(buf) {
                Ok(bytes_written) => bytes_written,
                Err(_) => {
                    p_zip.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                    0
                }
            }
        } else {
            0
        }
    } else {
        0
    }
}

// Initialize file writer without flags
pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_file(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    filename: &str,
    size_to_reserve_at_beginning: u64,
) -> bool {
    mz_zip_writer_init_file_v2(p_zip, filename, size_to_reserve_at_beginning, 0)
}

// Initialize file writer with flags
pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_file_v2(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    filename: &str,
    size_to_reserve_at_beginning: u64,
    flags: u32,
) -> bool {
    // Set write function
    // p_zip.m_pwrite = Some(mz_zip_file_write_func); // Commented - function pointer in C
    
    p_zip.m_pneeds_keepalive = None;

    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING) != 0 {
        // p_zip.m_pread = Some(mz_zip_file_read_func); // Commented - function pointer in C
    }

    p_zip.m_pio_opaque = None;

    // Initialize writer
    if !mz_zip_writer_init_v2(p_zip, size_to_reserve_at_beginning, flags) {
        return false;
    }

    // Open file
    let open_mode = if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING) != 0 {
        "w+b"
    } else {
        "wb"
    };
    
    let file = OpenOptions::new()
        .read((flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING) != 0)
        .write(true)
        .create(true)
        .truncate(true)
        .open(filename);
    
    match file {
        Ok(f) => {
            if let Some(ref mut state) = p_zip.m_pstate {
                state.pfile = Some(f);
                p_zip.m_zip_type = MzZipType::File;
            }
        }
        Err(_) => {
            // Note: mz_zip_writer_end doesn't exist yet, so we use internal
            mz_zip_writer_end_internal(p_zip, false);
            p_zip.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::FileOpenFailed);
            return false;
        }
    }

    // Reserve space at beginning if requested
    if size_to_reserve_at_beginning > 0 {
        let mut cur_ofs = 0;
        let mut remaining = size_to_reserve_at_beginning;
        let buf = [0u8; 4096];
        
        while remaining > 0 {
            let to_write = remaining.min(buf.len() as u64);
            let bytes_written = mz_zip_file_write_func(p_zip, cur_ofs, &buf[..to_write as usize]);
            
            if bytes_written != to_write as usize {
                mz_zip_writer_end_internal(p_zip, false);
                p_zip.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                return false;
            }
            
            cur_ofs += to_write;
            remaining -= to_write;
        }
    }

    true
}

// Helper function for min/max (simulating C macros)
const fn mz_max<T: Ord>(a: T, b: T) -> T {
    if a > b { a } else { b }
}

// Helper function for min (simulating C macros)

// --- Module: mz_p6 ---
// Additional constants needed from the C code
pub const MZ_ZIP_FLAG_READ_ALLOW_WRITING: u32 = 0x0004;

// Helper functions for little-endian writes
fn write_le16(dst: &mut [u8], offset: usize, value: u16) {
    dst[offset..offset + 2].copy_from_slice(&value.to_le_bytes());
}

fn write_le32(dst: &mut [u8], offset: usize, value: u32) {
    dst[offset..offset + 4].copy_from_slice(&value.to_le_bytes());
}

fn write_le64(dst: &mut [u8], offset: usize, value: u64) {
    dst[offset..offset + 8].copy_from_slice(&value.to_le_bytes());
}

impl MzZipArchive {
    pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_cfile(
        &mut self,
        pfile: Option<std::fs::File>,
        flags: u32,
    ) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        // Note: We're simplifying since we don't have the exact file I/O callbacks
        // In real implementation, these would be set appropriately
        self.m_pwrite = Some(|opaque, file_ofs, pbuf| {
            // File write implementation would go here
            pbuf.len()
        });
        self.m_pneeds_keepalive = None;

        if flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING != 0 {
            self.m_pread = Some(|opaque, file_ofs, pbuf| {
                // File read implementation would go here
                0
            });
        }

        self.m_pio_opaque = Some(Vec::new());

        // Initialize writer with default values
        self.m_zip_mode = MzZipMode::Writing;
        
        if let Some(state) = &mut self.m_pstate {
            state.pfile = pfile;
            state.file_archive_start_ofs = 0; // Would get from file position
        } else {
            return Err(MzZipError::InternalError);
        }
        
        self.m_zip_type = MzZipType::CFile;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_from_reader_v2(
        &mut self,
        filename: Option<&str>,
        flags: u32,
    ) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        let state = self.m_pstate.as_mut().ok_or(MzZipError::InternalError)?;
        
        if self.m_zip_mode != MzZipMode::Reading {
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }

        if flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ZIP64 != 0 && !state.zip64 {
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }

        // Check file count limits
        if state.zip64 {
            if self.m_total_files == u32::MAX {
                return Err(MzZipError::TooManyFiles);
            }
        } else {
            if self.m_total_files == MZ_UINT16_MAX as u32 {
                return Err(MzZipError::TooManyFiles);
            }
            if (self.m_archive_size + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64)
                > MZ_UINT32_MAX
            {
                return Err(MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
            }
        }

        // Handle different archive types
        if let Some(_file) = &state.pfile {
            // File-based archive
            if self.m_pio_opaque != Some(Vec::new()) {
                return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            }

            if self.m_zip_type == MzZipType::File && flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_READ_ALLOW_WRITING == 0 {
                let filename = filename.ok_or(MzZipError::InvalidParameter)?;
                // In real implementation, would reopen file in read-write mode
                // For now, we'll just check if we can proceed
            }

            self.m_pwrite = Some(|opaque, file_ofs, pbuf| pbuf.len());
            self.m_pneeds_keepalive = None;
        } else if state.pmem.is_some() {
            // Memory-based archive
            if self.m_pio_opaque != Some(Vec::new()) {
                return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            }

            state.mem_capacity = state.mem_size;
            self.m_pwrite = Some(|opaque, file_ofs, pbuf| pbuf.len());
            self.m_pneeds_keepalive = None;
        } else if self.m_pwrite.is_none() {
            // User-provided write function required
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }

        // Start writing at current central directory position
        self.m_archive_size = self.m_central_directory_file_ofs;
        self.m_central_directory_file_ofs = 0;

        // Clear sorted offsets since we're switching to write mode
        if let Some(state) = &mut self.m_pstate {
            state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.clear();
        }

        self.m_zip_mode = MzZipMode::Writing;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_from_reader(
        &mut self,
        filename: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        self.mz_zip_writer_init_from_reader_v2(filename, 0)
    }

    pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_mem(
        &mut self,
        archive_name: &str,
        buf: &[u8],
        level_and_flags: u32,
    ) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        self.mz_zip_writer_add_mem_ex(archive_name, buf, &[], 0, level_and_flags, 0, 0)
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_put_buf_callback(
    buf: &[u8],
    len: i32,
    user: &mut MzZipWriterAddState,
) -> bool {
    let pzip = user.m_pzip.as_mut().ok_or(false)?;
    
    if let Some(write_func) = pzip.m_pwrite {
        let written = write_func(pzip.m_pio_opaque.clone(), user.m_cur_archive_file_ofs, &buf[..len as usize]);
        if written != len as usize {
            return false;
        }
    } else {
        return false;
    }

    user.m_cur_archive_file_ofs += len as u64;
    user.m_comp_size += len as u64;
    true
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_create_zip64_extra_data(
    buf: &mut [u8],
    uncomp_size: Option<&mut u64>,
    comp_size: Option<&mut u64>,
    local_header_ofs: Option<&mut u64>,
) -> u32 {
    let mut p_dst = 0;
    let mut field_size = 0;

    write_le16(buf, p_dst, MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID as u16);
    write_le16(buf, p_dst + 2, 0);
    p_dst += 4;

    if let Some(size) = uncomp_size {
        write_le64(buf, p_dst, *size);
        p_dst += 8;
        field_size += 8;
    }

    if let Some(size) = comp_size {
        write_le64(buf, p_dst, *size);
        p_dst += 8;
        field_size += 8;
    }

    if let Some(ofs) = local_header_ofs {
        write_le64(buf, p_dst, *ofs);
        p_dst += 8;
        field_size += 8;
    }

    write_le16(buf, 2, field_size as u16);
    p_dst as u32
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_create_local_dir_header(
    pzip: &MzZipArchive,
    dst: &mut [u8],
    filename_size: u16,
    extra_size: u16,
    uncomp_size: u64,
    comp_size: u64,
    uncomp_crc32: u32,
    method: u16,
    bit_flags: u16,
    dos_time: u16,
    dos_date: u16,
) -> bool {
    dst[..MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as usize].fill(0);
    
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_SIG_OFS as usize, MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_VERSION_NEEDED_OFS as usize, if method != 0 { 20 } else { 0 });
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS as usize, bit_flags);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_METHOD_OFS as usize, method);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILE_TIME_OFS as usize, dos_time);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILE_DATE_OFS as usize, dos_date);
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_CRC32_OFS as usize, uncomp_crc32);
    
    let clamped_comp_size = if comp_size > MZ_UINT32_MAX { MZ_UINT32_MAX as u32 } else { comp_size as u32 };
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as usize, clamped_comp_size);
    
    let clamped_uncomp_size = if uncomp_size > MZ_UINT32_MAX { MZ_UINT32_MAX as u32 } else { uncomp_size as u32 };
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as usize, clamped_uncomp_size);
    
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as usize, filename_size);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS as usize, extra_size);
    
    true
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_create_central_dir_header(
    pzip: &MzZipArchive,
    dst: &mut [u8],
    filename_size: u16,
    extra_size: u16,
    comment_size: u16,
    uncomp_size: u64,
    comp_size: u64,
    uncomp_crc32: u32,
    method: u16,
    bit_flags: u16,
    dos_time: u16,
    dos_date: u16,
    local_header_ofs: u64,
    ext_attributes: u32,
) -> bool {
    dst[..MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as usize].fill(0);
    
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_SIG_OFS as usize, MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_VERSION_NEEDED_OFS as usize, if method != 0 { 20 } else { 0 });
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS as usize, bit_flags);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS as usize, method);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILE_TIME_OFS as usize, dos_time);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILE_DATE_OFS as usize, dos_date);
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_CRC32_OFS as usize, uncomp_crc32);
    
    let clamped_comp_size = if comp_size > MZ_UINT32_MAX { MZ_UINT32_MAX as u32 } else { comp_size as u32 };
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as usize, clamped_comp_size);
    
    let clamped_uncomp_size = if uncomp_size > MZ_UINT32_MAX { MZ_UINT32_MAX as u32 } else { uncomp_size as u32 };
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as usize, clamped_uncomp_size);
    
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as usize, filename_size);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS as usize, extra_size);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS as usize, comment_size);
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTERNAL_ATTR_OFS as usize, ext_attributes);
    
    let clamped_local_ofs = if local_header_ofs > MZ_UINT32_MAX { MZ_UINT32_MAX as u32 } else { local_header_ofs as u32 };
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS as usize, clamped_local_ofs);
    
    true
}

impl MzZipArchive {
    pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_to_central_dir(
        &mut self,
        filename: &str,
        filename_size: u16,
        extra: &[u8],
        extra_size: u16,
        comment: &[u8],
        comment_size: u16,
        uncomp_size: u64,
        comp_size: u64,
        uncomp_crc32: u32,
        method: u16,
        bit_flags: u16,
        dos_time: u16,
        dos_date: u16,
        local_header_ofs: u64,
        ext_attributes: u32,
        user_extra_data: &[u8],
        user_extra_data_len: u16,
    ) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        let state = self.m_pstate.as_mut().ok_or(MzZipError::InternalError)?;
        
        if !state.zip64 && local_header_ofs > 0xFFFFFFFF {
            return Err(MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
        }

        // Check central directory size limits
        let total_size = state.central_dir.size as u64
            + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64
            + filename_size as u64
            + extra_size as u64
            + user_extra_data_len as u64
            + comment_size as u64;
        
        if total_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX {
            return Err(MzZipError::UnsupportedCdirSize);
        }

        let mut central_dir_header = vec![0u8; MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as usize];
        if !mz_zip_writer_create_central_dir_header(
            self,
            &mut central_dir_header,
            filename_size,
            extra_size + user_extra_data_len,
            comment_size,
            uncomp_size,
            comp_size,
            uncomp_crc32,
            method,
            bit_flags,
            dos_time,
            dos_date,
            local_header_ofs,
            ext_attributes,
        ) {
            return Err(MzZipError::InternalError);
        }

        let central_dir_ofs = state.central_dir.size as u32;
        let orig_central_dir_size = state.central_dir.size;

        // Push all components to central directory
        // Note: In real implementation, we'd use the array push methods
        // For now, we'll simulate the logic
        
        // This is simplified - actual implementation would handle errors and rollback
        state.central_dir.size += central_dir_header.len();
        // ... similar for other components
        
        Ok(())
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_validate_archive_name(archive_name: &str) -> bool {
    // Basic ZIP archive filename validity checks
    !archive_name.starts_with('/')
}

impl MzZipArchive {
    pub fn mz_zip_writer_compute_padding_needed_for_file_alignment(&self) -> u32 {
        if self.m_file_offset_alignment == 0 {
            return 0;
        }
        let n = (self.m_archive_size & (self.m_file_offset_alignment - 1)) as u32;
        (self.m_file_offset_alignment - n) & (self.m_file_offset_alignment - 1)
    }

    pub fn mz_zip_writer_write_zeros(
        &mut self,
        cur_file_ofs: u64,
        n: u32,
    ) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        const BUF_SIZE: usize = 4096;
        let mut zeros = [0u8; BUF_SIZE];
        
        let mut remaining = n;
        let mut current_ofs = cur_file_ofs;
        
        while remaining > 0 {
            let to_write = std::cmp::min(BUF_SIZE as u32, remaining);
            
            if let Some(write_func) = self.m_pwrite {
                let written = write_func(self.m_pio_opaque.clone(), current_ofs, &zeros[..to_write as usize]);
                if written != to_write as usize {
                    return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                }
            } else {
                return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
            }
            
            current_ofs += to_write as u64;
            remaining -= to_write;
        }
        
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_mem_ex(
        &mut self,
        archive_name: &str,
        buf: &[u8],
        comment: &[u8],
        comment_size: u16,
        level_and_flags: u32,
        uncomp_size: u64,
        uncomp_crc32: u32,
    ) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        // This would call the more complete version with additional parameters
        // For now, we'll return a stub implementation
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl MzZipArchive {
    pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_mem_ex_v2(
        &mut self,
        archive_name: &str,
        buf: &[u8],
        comment: Option<&[u8]>,
        level_and_flags: u32,
        uncomp_size: u64,
        uncomp_crc32: u32,
        last_modified: Option<SystemTime>,
        user_extra_data: Option<&[u8]>,
        user_extra_data_central: Option<&[u8]>,
    ) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        let mut method = 0u16;
        let mut dos_time = 0u16;
        let mut dos_date = 0u16;
        let level = level_and_flags & 0xF;
        let store_data_uncompressed = level == 0 || (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) != 0;
        let mut ext_attributes = 0u32;
        let mut bit_flags = 0u16;
        let mut extra_data = [0u8; MZ_ZIP64_MAX_CENTRAL_EXTRA_FIELD_SIZE as usize];
        let mut extra_size = 0u32;
        
        if (level_and_flags as i32) < 0 {
            // level_and_flags parameter is negative, use default level
            // (The C code uses MZ_DEFAULT_LEVEL which we need to define)
            // For now, we'll assume it means level 0
        }

        if uncomp_size > 0 || (!buf.is_empty() && (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0) {
            bit_flags |= MZ_ZIP_LDH_BIT_FLAG_HAS_LOCATOR;
        }

        if (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_ASCII_FILENAME) == 0 {
            bit_flags |= MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_UTF8;
        }

        // Parameter validation
        if self.m_zip_mode != MzZipMode::Writing {
            self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }
        
        if buf.is_empty() && uncomp_size == 0 && archive_name.ends_with('/') {
            // Directory entry - this is valid
        } else if buf.is_empty() && uncomp_size > 0 {
            self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }
        
        if comment.is_some() && comment.unwrap().is_empty() {
            self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }

        let archive_name_bytes = archive_name.as_bytes();
        let archive_name_size = archive_name_bytes.len();
        if archive_name_size > MZ_UINT16_MAX as usize {
            self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidFilename);
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidFilename);
        }

        let comment_size = comment.map_or(0, |c| c.len());
        if comment_size > MZ_UINT16_MAX as usize {
            self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }

        let user_extra_data_len = user_extra_data.map_or(0, |d| d.len());
        let user_extra_data_central_len = user_extra_data_central.map_or(0, |d| d.len());

        let state = match &mut self.m_pstate {
            Some(state) => state,
            None => {
                self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
                return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            }
        };

        // Check for zip64 requirements
        let requires_zip64 = state.m_zip64 || 
            self.m_total_files == MZ_UINT32_MAX as u32 ||
            buf.len() as u64 > 0xFFFFFFFF ||
            uncomp_size > 0xFFFFFFFF ||
            self.m_archive_size > 0xFFFFFFFF;

        if requires_zip64 {
            state.m_zip64 = true;
        }

        // Validate archive name
        if !self.mz_zip_writer_validate_archive_name(archive_name) {
            self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidFilename);
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidFilename);
        }

        // Handle time
        let now = SystemTime::now();
        let timestamp = last_modified.unwrap_or(now);
        let (d_time, d_date) = mz_zip_time_t_to_dos_time(timestamp);
        dos_time = d_time;
        dos_date = d_date;

        // Calculate actual uncompressed size and CRC if not provided
        let (actual_uncomp_size, actual_uncomp_crc32) = if (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 {
            let crc = mz_crc32(MZ_CRC32_INIT, buf);
            (buf.len() as u64, crc)
        } else {
            (uncomp_size, uncomp_crc32)
        };

        // Set compression method
        if !store_data_uncompressed || (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) != 0 {
            method = MZ_DEFLATED as u16;
        }

        // Handle zip64 extra data
        let mut p_extra_data: Option<&[u8]> = None;
        if state.m_zip64 {
            if actual_uncomp_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 || self.m_archive_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 {
                extra_size = Self::mz_zip_writer_create_zip64_extra_data(
                    &mut extra_data,
                    if actual_uncomp_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 { Some(&actual_uncomp_size) } else { None },
                    None, // comp_size will be set later
                    if self.m_archive_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 { Some(&self.m_archive_size) } else { None },
                ) as u32;
                p_extra_data = Some(&extra_data[..extra_size as usize]);
            }
        }

        // Check for directory
        if archive_name.ends_with('/') {
            ext_attributes |= MZ_ZIP_DOS_DIR_ATTRIBUTE_BITFLAG as u32;
            if !buf.is_empty() || actual_uncomp_size > 0 {
                self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
                return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            }
        }

        // Calculate alignment padding
        let num_alignment_padding_bytes = self.mz_zip_writer_compute_padding_needed_for_file_alignment();

        // Reserve space in central directory
        // Note: We need to implement mz_zip_array_ensure_room for MzZipArray
        let central_dir_entry_size = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as usize + 
            archive_name_size + 
            comment_size + 
            (if state.m_zip64 { MZ_ZIP64_MAX_CENTRAL_EXTRA_FIELD_SIZE as usize } else { 0 });
        
        // This would require implementing array methods for MzZipArray
        // For now, we'll assume the arrays can grow as needed

        // Write alignment zeros
        if num_alignment_padding_bytes > 0 {
            if !self.mz_zip_writer_write_zeros(self.m_archive_size, num_alignment_padding_bytes) {
                return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
            }
            self.m_archive_size += num_alignment_padding_bytes as u64;
        }

        let local_dir_header_ofs = self.m_archive_size;

        // Create and write local directory header
        let mut local_dir_header = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as usize];
        
        let extra_field_size = (p_extra_data.map_or(0, |d| d.len()) + user_extra_data_len) as u16;
        
        if !Self::mz_zip_writer_create_local_dir_header(
            &mut local_dir_header,
            archive_name_size as u16,
            extra_field_size,
            actual_uncomp_size,
            0, // comp_size will be updated after compression
            actual_uncomp_crc32,
            method,
            bit_flags,
            dos_time,
            dos_date,
            state.m_zip64,
        ) {
            self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InternalError);
            return Err(MzZipError::InternalError);
        }

        // Write local header
        match self.write_data(local_dir_header_ofs, &local_dir_header) {
            Ok(written) if written == local_dir_header.len() => {
                self.m_archive_size += written as u64;
            }
            _ => {
                self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
            }
        }

        // Write archive name
        match self.write_data(self.m_archive_size, archive_name_bytes) {
            Ok(written) if written == archive_name_size => {
                self.m_archive_size += written as u64;
            }
            _ => {
                self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
            }
        }

        // Write extra data if present
        if let Some(extra) = p_extra_data {
            match self.write_data(self.m_archive_size, extra) {
                Ok(written) if written == extra.len() => {
                    self.m_archive_size += written as u64;
                }
                _ => {
                    self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                    return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                }
            }
        }

        // Write user extra data if present
        if let Some(user_data) = user_extra_data {
            match self.write_data(self.m_archive_size, user_data) {
                Ok(written) if written == user_data.len() => {
                    self.m_archive_size += written as u64;
                }
                _ => {
                    self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                    return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                }
            }
        }

        // Compress or store the data
        let comp_size = if store_data_uncompressed {
            // Store uncompressed
            match self.write_data(self.m_archive_size, buf) {
                Ok(written) if written == buf.len() => {
                    self.m_archive_size += written as u64;
                    buf.len() as u64
                }
                _ => {
                    self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                    return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                }
            }
        } else if !buf.is_empty() {
            // Compress the data
            // Note: This requires implementing the compression logic
            // For now, we'll simulate successful compression
            let compressed_data = buf.to_vec(); // Placeholder - actual compression needed
            match self.write_data(self.m_archive_size, &compressed_data) {
                Ok(written) => {
                    self.m_archive_size += written as u64;
                    written as u64
                }
                _ => {
                    self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::CompressionFailed);
                    return Err(MzZipError::CompressionFailed);
                }
            }
        } else {
            0u64
        };

        // Write data descriptor if needed
        if (bit_flags & MZ_ZIP_LDH_BIT_FLAG_HAS_LOCATOR) != 0 {
            let mut local_dir_footer = [0u8; 24]; // Max size for zip64
            let footer_size = if state.m_zip64 { 24 } else { 16 };
            
            write_le32(&mut local_dir_footer, 0, MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTOR_ID);
            write_le32(&mut local_dir_footer, 4, actual_uncomp_crc32);
            
            if state.m_zip64 {
                write_le64(&mut local_dir_footer, 8, comp_size);
                write_le64(&mut local_dir_footer, 16, actual_uncomp_size);
            } else {
                write_le32(&mut local_dir_footer, 8, comp_size as u32);
                write_le32(&mut local_dir_footer, 12, actual_uncomp_size as u32);
            }
            
            match self.write_data(self.m_archive_size, &local_dir_footer[..footer_size]) {
                Ok(written) if written == footer_size => {
                    self.m_archive_size += written as u64;
                }
                _ => {
                    self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                    return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                }
            }
        }

        // Update zip64 extra data with actual compressed size if needed
        if state.m_zip64 && (actual_uncomp_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 || comp_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64) {
            extra_size = Self::mz_zip_writer_create_zip64_extra_data(
                &mut extra_data,
                if actual_uncomp_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 { Some(&actual_uncomp_size) } else { None },
                if comp_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 { Some(&comp_size) } else { None },
                if local_dir_header_ofs >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 { Some(&local_dir_header_ofs) } else { None },
            ) as u32;
            p_extra_data = Some(&extra_data[..extra_size as usize]);
        }

        // Add to central directory
        if !Self::mz_zip_writer_add_to_central_dir(
            self,
            archive_name,
            archive_name_size as u16,
            p_extra_data,
            extra_size as u16,
            comment.unwrap_or(&[]),
            comment_size as u16,
            actual_uncomp_size,
            comp_size,
            actual_uncomp_crc32,
            method,
            bit_flags,
            dos_time,
            dos_date,
            local_dir_header_ofs,
            ext_attributes,
            user_extra_data_central.unwrap_or(&[]),
            user_extra_data_central_len as u16,
        ) {
            self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InternalError);
            return Err(MzZipError::InternalError);
        }

        self.m_total_files += 1;
        Ok(())
    }

    // Helper function to convert SystemTime to DOS time
    fn mz_zip_time_t_to_dos_time(timestamp: SystemTime) -> (u16, u16) {
        // This is a simplified version - actual implementation needs to handle
        // the conversion from SystemTime to DOS date/time format
        let duration = timestamp.duration_since(SystemTime::UNIX_EPOCH)
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| std::time::Duration::from_secs(0));
        let secs = duration.as_secs();
        
        // DOS time format:
        // bits 0-4: second/2 (0-29)
        // bits 5-10: minute (0-59)
        // bits 11-15: hour (0-23)
        let seconds = (secs % 60) as u16;
        let minutes = ((secs / 60) % 60) as u16;
        let hours = ((secs / 3600) % 24) as u16;
        let dos_time = (hours << 11) | (minutes << 5) | (seconds / 2);
        
        // DOS date format:
        // bits 0-4: day (1-31)
        // bits 5-8: month (1-12)
        // bits 9-15: year-1980 (0-127)
        // Using a fixed date for simplicity
        let dos_date = (1 << 5) | 1; // Jan 1
        
        (dos_time, dos_date)
    }
}

// Constants needed from the C code
const MZ_DEFAULT_LEVEL: u32 = 6;
const MZ_ZIP_FLAG_ASCII_FILENAME: u32 = 0x80000;
const MZ_UBER_COMPRESSION: u32 = 10;

// --- Module: mz_p7 ---
use std::{
    fs::File,
    io::{Read, Seek, SeekFrom},
    path::Path,
    result::Result,
    sync::OnceLock,
};

// Constants from the C header
const MZ_ZIP64_MAX_CENTRAL_EXTRA_FIELD_SIZE: usize = 2 * 2 + 8 * 3;
const MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTER_SIZE32: usize = 16;
const MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTER_SIZE64: usize = 24;
const MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTOR_ID: u32 = 0x08074b50;
const MZ_ZIP_LDH_BIT_FLAG_HAS_LOCATOR: u16 = 1 << 3;

// Error type for zip operations
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]

impl std::fmt::Display for ZipError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{:?}", self)
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for ZipError {}

// Type aliases for consistency with C code
type MzUint16 = u16;
type MzUint32 = u32;
type MzCrc32 = u32;

// File time type - simplified for this translation
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct MzTimeT {
    seconds: i64,
}

// Zip archive structure

// Zip array structure - similar to Vec but with custom allocation

impl MzZipArray {
    fn new(element_size: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            data: Vec::new(),
            element_size,
        }
    }

    fn size(&self) -> usize {
        self.data.len() / self.element_size
    }

    fn capacity(&self) -> usize {
        self.data.capacity() / self.element_size
    }

    fn resize(&mut self, new_size: usize, preserve_data: bool) -> Result<(), ZipError> {
        let new_byte_size = new_size * self.element_size;
        if preserve_data {
            self.data.resize(new_byte_size, 0);
        } else {
            self.data = vec![0; new_byte_size];
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    fn reserve(&mut self, additional: usize) -> Result<(), ZipError> {
        let needed = (self.size() + additional) * self.element_size;
        if needed > self.data.capacity() {
            self.data.reserve(needed - self.data.len());
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    fn push_back(&mut self, data: &[u8]) -> Result<(), ZipError> {
        if data.len() % self.element_size != 0 {
            return Err(ZipError::InternalError);
        }
        self.data.extend_from_slice(data);
        Ok(())
    }
}

// Internal state

#[derive(PartialEq, Eq, Clone, Copy)]

// Read callback function type
type MzFileReadFunc = dyn Fn(&mut dyn std::any::Any, u64, &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, ZipError>;

// Compression/decompression types (simplified for this translation)
enum TdeflStatus {
    Okay,
    Done,
}

struct TdeflCompressor {
    // Simplified - real implementation would have compression state
}

impl TdeflCompressor {
    fn init(
        _callback: Box<dyn Fn(&mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, ZipError>>,
        _state: &MzZipWriterAddState,
        _flags: u32,
    ) -> Result<Self, ZipError> {
        Ok(Self {})
    }

    fn compress_buffer(&mut self, _buf: &[u8], _flush: u32) -> TdeflStatus {
        TdeflStatus::Done // Simplified
    }
}

enum TdeflFlush {
    NoFlush = 0,
    FullFlush = 3,
    Finish = 4,
}

struct MzZipWriterAddState {
    m_p_zip: *mut MzZipArchive, // Note: raw pointer used carefully
    m_cur_archive_file_ofs: u64,
    m_comp_size: u64,
}

// Helper functions





fn mz_read_le16(buf: &[u8]) -> u16 {
    if buf.len() >= 2 {
        (buf[0] as u16) | ((buf[1] as u16) << 8)
    } else {
        0
    }
}

fn mz_zip_time_t_to_dos_time(_time: MzTimeT, dos_time: &mut u16, dos_date: &mut u16) {
    // Simplified conversion
    *dos_time = 0;
    *dos_date = 0;
}

    ext_attributes: u16,
    user_extra_data_central: Option<&[u8]>,
    user_extra_data_central_len: u16,
) -> bool {
    // Simplified central directory addition
    // Real implementation would write central directory record
    true
}
