    level as u32 // Simplified
}

// Main functions to translate

pub fn mz_file_read_func_stdio(
    p_opaque: &mut dyn std::any::Any,
    file_ofs: u64,
    p_buf: &mut [u8],
) -> Result<usize, ZipError> {
    // Try to cast to File
    let file = p_opaque.downcast_mut::<File>()
        .ok_or(ZipError::InvalidParameter)?;
    
    // Seek to position
    if file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(file_ofs)).is_err() {
        return Ok(0);
    }
    
    // Read data
    match file.read(p_buf) {
        Ok(n) => Ok(n),
        Err(_) => Ok(0),
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_file(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    p_archive_name: &str,
    p_src_filename: &Path,
    p_comment: Option<&[u8]>,
    comment_size: u16,
    level_and_flags: u32,
) -> Result<bool, ZipError> {
    // Open source file
    let mut src_file = File::open(p_src_filename)
        .map_err(|_| ZipError::FileOpenFailed)?;
    
    // Get file size
    let uncomp_size = src_file
        .metadata()
        .map_err(|_| ZipError::FileStatFailed)?
        .len();
    
    // Get modified time (simplified)
    let mut file_modified_time = MzTimeT { seconds: 0 };
    #[cfg(not(feature = "no_time"))]
    {
        if let Ok(metadata) = src_file.metadata() {
            if let Ok(modified) = metadata.modified() {
                file_modified_time.seconds = modified
                    .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
                    .unwrap_or_default()
                    .as_secs() as i64;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Call the cfile version
    let result = mz_zip_writer_add_cfile(
        p_zip,
        p_archive_name,
        &mut src_file,
        uncomp_size,
        Some(&file_modified_time),
        p_comment,
        comment_size,
        level_and_flags,
        None,
        0,
        None,
        0,
    );
    
    src_file.sync_all().ok(); // Ensure all data is written
    
    result
}

fn mz_zip_writer_add_cfile(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    p_archive_name: &str,
    p_src_file: &mut File,
    max_size: u64,
    p_file_time: Option<&MzTimeT>,
    p_comment: Option<&[u8]>,
    comment_size: u16,
    level_and_flags: u32,
    user_extra_data: Option<&[u8]>,
    user_extra_data_len: u16,
    user_extra_data_central: Option<&[u8]>,
    user_extra_data_central_len: u16,
) -> Result<bool, ZipError> {
    // Wrap the file in a closure for the callback
    let callback = Box::new(|opaque: &mut dyn std::any::Any, file_ofs: u64, buf: &mut [u8]| {
        let file = opaque.downcast_mut::<File>()
            .ok_or(ZipError::InvalidParameter)?;
        mz_file_read_func_stdio(file, file_ofs, buf)
    });
    
    mz_zip_writer_add_read_buf_callback(
        p_zip,
        p_archive_name,
        callback,
        p_src_file,
        max_size,
        p_file_time,
        p_comment,
        comment_size,
        level_and_flags,
        user_extra_data,
        user_extra_data_len,
        user_extra_data_central,
        user_extra_data_central_len,
    )
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_read_buf_callback(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    p_archive_name: &str,
    read_callback: Box<MzFileReadFunc>,
    callback_opaque: &mut dyn std::any::Any,
    max_size: u64,
    p_file_time: Option<&MzTimeT>,
    p_comment: Option<&[u8]>,
    comment_size: u16,
    level_and_flags: u32,
    user_extra_data: Option<&[u8]>,
    user_extra_data_len: u16,
    user_extra_data_central: Option<&[u8]>,
    user_extra_data_central_len: u16,
) -> Result<bool, ZipError> {
    // Parameter validation
    if p_zip.m_zip_mode != ZipMode::Writing {
        return Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    if p_archive_name.is_empty() {
        return Err(ZipError::InvalidFilename);
    }
    
    if comment_size > 0 && p_comment.is_none() {
        return Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    let level = if (level_and_flags as i32) < 0 {
        MZ_DEFAULT_LEVEL
    } else {
        level_and_flags & 0xF
    };
    
    if level > MZ_UBER_COMPRESSION {
        return Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    // Get internal state
    let p_state = p_zip.m_p_state.get_mut()
        .ok_or(ZipError::InternalError)?;
    
    // Check for zip64 requirements
    if !p_state.m_zip64 && max_size > MZ_UINT32_MAX {
        p_state.m_zip64 = true;
    }
    
    if level_and_flags & 0x8000 != 0 { // MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA
        return Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    if !mz_zip_writer_validate_archive_name(p_archive_name) {
        return Err(ZipError::InvalidFilename);
    }
    
    // Check file count limits
    if p_state.m_zip64 {
        if p_zip.m_total_files == MZ_UINT32_MAX {
            return Err(ZipError::TooManyFiles);
        }
    } else if p_zip.m_total_files == MZ_UINT16_MAX as u32 {
        p_state.m_zip64 = true;
    }
    
    let archive_name_size = p_archive_name.len();
    if archive_name_size > MZ_UINT16_MAX as usize {
        return Err(ZipError::InvalidFilename);
    }
    
    // Compute padding
    let num_alignment_padding_bytes = mz_zip_writer_compute_padding_needed_for_file_alignment(p_zip);
    
    // Check central directory size
    let cdir_est_size = p_state.m_central_dir.size() + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE 
        + archive_name_size + MZ_ZIP64_MAX_CENTRAL_EXTRA_FIELD_SIZE 
        + comment_size as usize;
    
    if cdir_est_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as usize {
        return Err(ZipError::UnsupportedCdirSize);
    }
    
    // DOS time/date
    let (mut dos_time, mut dos_date) = (0u16, 0u16);
    if let Some(file_time) = p_file_time {
        mz_zip_time_t_to_dos_time(*file_time, &mut dos_time, &mut dos_date);
    }
    
    // Determine compression method
    let method = if max_size > 0 && level > 0 { 8 } else { 0 }; // 8 = DEFLATE
    
    // Write alignment padding
    if !mz_zip_writer_write_zeros(p_zip, p_zip.m_archive_size, num_alignment_padding_bytes) {
        return Err(ZipError::FileWriteFailed);
    }
    
    let mut cur_archive_file_ofs = p_zip.m_archive_size + num_alignment_padding_bytes as u64;
    let local_dir_header_ofs = cur_archive_file_ofs;
    
    // Create and write local directory header
    let mut local_dir_header = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
    let mut extra_data = [0u8; MZ_ZIP64_MAX_CENTRAL_EXTRA_FIELD_SIZE];
    let mut extra_size = 0;
    
    if p_state.m_zip64 {
        // Handle zip64 extra data
        if max_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX || local_dir_header_ofs >= MZ_UINT32_MAX {
            extra_size = mz_zip_writer_create_zip64_extra_data(
                &mut extra_data,
                if max_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX { Some(&max_size) } else { None },
                if max_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX { Some(&max_size) } else { None },
                if local_dir_header_ofs >= MZ_UINT32_MAX { Some(&local_dir_header_ofs) } else { None },
            );
        }
    }
    
    // Write the file data
    let mut uncomp_crc32 = 0u32;
    let mut uncomp_size = 0u64;
    let mut comp_size = 0u64;
    
    if max_size > 0 {
        if level == 0 {
            // Store uncompressed
            let mut read_buf = vec![0u8; MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE];
            let mut file_ofs = 0u64;
            
            loop {
                let n = read_callback(callback_opaque, file_ofs, &mut read_buf[..])
                    .map_err(|_| ZipError::FileReadFailed)?;
                
                if n == 0 {
                    break;
                }
                
                if n > MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE || file_ofs + n as u64 > max_size {
                    return Err(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
                }
                
                // Write to archive
                let written = (p_zip.m_p_write)(
                    &mut *p_zip.m_p_io_opaque,
                    cur_archive_file_ofs,
                    &read_buf[..n],
                ).map_err(|_| ZipError::FileWriteFailed)?;
                
                if written != n {
                    return Err(ZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                }
                
                uncomp_crc32 = mz_crc32(uncomp_crc32, &read_buf[..n]);
                file_ofs += n as u64;
                cur_archive_file_ofs += n as u64;
            }
            
            uncomp_size = file_ofs;
            comp_size = uncomp_size;
        } else {
            // Compressed - simplified for this translation
            return Err(ZipError::CompressionFailed);
        }
    }
    
    // Write data descriptor if needed
    if level_and_flags & 0x2000 == 0 { // Not MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_HEADER_SET_SIZE
        let mut local_dir_footer = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTER_SIZE64];
        let local_dir_footer_size = if extra_size > 0 {
            MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTER_SIZE64
        } else {
            MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTER_SIZE32
        };
        
        mz_write_le32(&mut local_dir_footer[0..], MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTOR_ID);
        mz_write_le32(&mut local_dir_footer[4..], uncomp_crc32);
        
        if extra_size == 0 {
            if comp_size > MZ_UINT32_MAX {
                return Err(ZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
            }
            mz_write_le32(&mut local_dir_footer[8..], comp_size as u32);
            mz_write_le32(&mut local_dir_footer[12..], uncomp_size as u32);
        } else {
            mz_write_le64(&mut local_dir_footer[8..], comp_size);
            mz_write_le64(&mut local_dir_footer[16..], uncomp_size);
        }
        
        let written = (p_zip.m_p_write)(
            &mut *p_zip.m_p_io_opaque,
            cur_archive_file_ofs,
            &local_dir_footer[..local_dir_footer_size],
        ).map_err(|_| ZipError::FileWriteFailed)?;
        
        if written != local_dir_footer_size {
            return Ok(false);
        }
        
        cur_archive_file_ofs += local_dir_footer_size as u64;
    }
    
    // Add to central directory
    if !mz_zip_writer_add_to_central_dir(
        p_zip,
        p_archive_name,
        archive_name_size as u16,
        if extra_size > 0 { Some(&extra_data[..extra_size]) } else { None },
        extra_size as u16,
        p_comment,
        comment_size,
        uncomp_size,
        comp_size,
        uncomp_crc32,
        method,
        0, // gen_flags
        dos_time,
        dos_date,
        local_dir_header_ofs,
        0, // ext_attributes
        user_extra_data_central,
        user_extra_data_central_len,
    ) {
        return Ok(false);
    }
    
    p_zip.m_total_files += 1;
    p_zip.m_archive_size = cur_archive_file_ofs;
    
    Ok(true)
}

fn mz_zip_writer_update_zip64_extension_block(
    p_new_ext: &mut MzZipArray,
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    p_ext: Option<&[u8]>,
    ext_len: u32,
    p_comp_size: Option<&u64>,
    p_uncomp_size: Option<&u64>,
    p_local_header_ofs: Option<&u64>,
    p_disk_start: Option<&u32>,
) -> Result<bool, ZipError> {
    // Reserve space
    p_new_ext.reserve(ext_len as usize + 64)
        .map_err(|_| ZipError::AllocFailed)?;
    
    // Clear existing data
    p_new_ext.resize(0, false)?;
    
    // Create new zip64 extra field if needed
    if p_uncomp_size.is_some() || p_comp_size.is_some() || p_local_header_ofs.is_some() || p_disk_start.is_some() {
        let mut new_ext_block = [0u8; 64];
        let mut offset = 0;
        
        // Header ID
        mz_write_le16(&mut new_ext_block[offset..], MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID);
        offset += 2;
        
        // Placeholder for data size
        let size_pos = offset;
        offset += 2;
        
        let start_offset = offset;
        
        // Write fields
        if let Some(size) = p_uncomp_size {
            mz_write_le64(&mut new_ext_block[offset..], *size);
            offset += 8;
        }
        
        if let Some(size) = p_comp_size {
            mz_write_le64(&mut new_ext_block[offset..], *size);
            offset += 8;
        }
        
        if let Some(ofs) = p_local_header_ofs {
            mz_write_le64(&mut new_ext_block[offset..], *ofs);
            offset += 8;
        }
        
        if let Some(disk) = p_disk_start {
            mz_write_le32(&mut new_ext_block[offset..], *disk);
            offset += 4;
        }
        
        // Write actual data size
        let data_size = (offset - start_offset) as u16;
        mz_write_le16(&mut new_ext_block[size_pos..], data_size);
        
        p_new_ext.push_back(&new_ext_block[..offset])?;
    }
    
    // Copy other extra fields
    if let Some(p_ext) = p_ext {
        let mut extra_size_remaining = ext_len as usize;
        let mut p_extra_data = p_ext;
        
        while extra_size_remaining >= 4 {
            let field_id = mz_read_le16(p_extra_data);
            let field_data_size = mz_read_le16(&p_extra_data[2..]) as usize;
            let field_total_size = field_data_size + 4;
            
            if field_total_size > extra_size_remaining {
                return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
            }
            
            if field_id != MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID {
                p_new_ext.push_back(&p_extra_data[..field_total_size])?;
            }
            
            p_extra_data = &p_extra_data[field_total_size..];
            extra_size_remaining -= field_total_size;
        }
        
        if extra_size_remaining > 0 {
            return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
        }
    }
    
    Ok(true)
}

// --- Module: mz_p8 ---
// Constants from the C code

// Field offsets





// Error type
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]

impl fmt::Display for ZipError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{:?}", self)
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for ZipError {}


// Types
pub type mz_bool = bool;
pub type mz_uint = u32;
pub type mz_uint16 = u16;
pub type mz_uint32 = u32;
pub type mz_uint64 = u64;
pub type size_t = usize;

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct mz_zip_array {
    m_p: Vec<u8>,
    m_size: usize,
    m_capacity: usize,
    m_element_size: usize,
}

impl mz_zip_array {
    fn new(element_size: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            m_p: Vec::new(),
            m_size: 0,
            m_capacity: 0,
            m_element_size,
        }
    }

    fn resize(&mut self, new_size: usize, zero_new: bool) -> bool {
        if new_size > self.m_capacity {
            let new_capacity = new_size.max(self.m_capacity * 2);
            let mut new_vec = vec![0u8; new_capacity * self.m_element_size];
            
            if !self.m_p.is_empty() {
                let bytes_to_copy = self.m_size * self.m_element_size;
                new_vec[..bytes_to_copy].copy_from_slice(&self.m_p[..bytes_to_copy]);
            }
            
            self.m_p = new_vec;
            self.m_capacity = new_capacity;
        }
        
        if new_size > self.m_size && zero_new {
            let start = self.m_size * self.m_element_size;
            let end = new_size * self.m_element_size;
            self.m_p[start..end].fill(0);
        }
        
        self.m_size = new_size;
        true
    }

    fn clear(&mut self) {
        self.m_p.clear();
        self.m_size = 0;
        self.m_capacity = 0;
    }

    fn push_back<T: Copy>(&mut self, data: &[T]) -> bool {
        let data_bytes = unsafe {
            slice::from_raw_parts(
                data.as_ptr() as *const u8,
                data.len() * mem::size_of::<T>(),
            )
        };
        
        let old_size = self.m_size;
        if !self.resize(self.m_size + data.len(), false) {
            return false;
        }
        
        let dest_start = old_size * self.m_element_size;
        let dest_end = dest_start + data_bytes.len();
        
        // Ensure element sizes match
        if self.m_element_size != mem::size_of::<T>() {
            return false;
        }
        
        self.m_p[dest_start..dest_end].copy_from_slice(data_bytes);
        true
    }
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct mz_zip_internal_state {
    m_central_dir: mz_zip_array,
    m_central_dir_offsets: mz_zip_array,
    m_sorted_central_dir_offsets: mz_zip_array,
    m_init_flags: mz_uint32,
    m_zip64: bool,
    m_zip64_has_extended_info_fields: bool,
    m_file_archive_start_ofs: mz_uint64,
    m_mem_size: usize,
    m_mem_capacity: usize,
}

impl mz_zip_internal_state {
    fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            m_central_dir: mz_zip_array::new(1),
            m_central_dir_offsets: mz_zip_array::new(mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>()),
            m_sorted_central_dir_offsets: mz_zip_array::new(mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>()),
            m_init_flags: 0,
            m_zip64: false,
            m_zip64_has_extended_info_fields: false,
            m_file_archive_start_ofs: 0,
            m_mem_size: 0,
            m_mem_capacity: 0,
        }
    }
}

// I/O traits for abstraction
trait ZipIO: Send + Sync {
    fn read(&mut self, offset: u64, buf: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize>;
    fn write(&mut self, offset: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> Result<usize>;
    fn size(&self) -> u64;
}

// File-based I/O
struct FileZipIO {
    file: File,
}

impl FileZipIO {
    fn new(file: File) -> Self {
        Self { file }
    }
}

impl ZipIO for FileZipIO {
    fn read(&mut self, offset: u64, buf: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize> {
        self.file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(offset))
            .map_err(|_| ZipError::FileReadFailed)?;
        self.file.read(buf).map_err(|_| ZipError::FileReadFailed)
    }

    fn write(&mut self, offset: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> Result<usize> {
        self.file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(offset))
            .map_err(|_| ZipError::FileWriteFailed)?;
        self.file.write(buf).map_err(|_| ZipError::FileWriteFailed)
    }

    fn size(&self) -> u64 {
        self.file.metadata().map(|m| m.len()).unwrap_or(0)
    }
}

// Memory-based I/O
struct MemoryZipIO {
    data: Vec<u8>,
}

impl MemoryZipIO {
    fn new() -> Self {
        Self { data: Vec::new() }
    }
}

impl ZipIO for MemoryZipIO {
    fn read(&mut self, offset: u64, buf: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize> {
        let offset = offset as usize;
        if offset >= self.data.len() {
            return Ok(0);
        }
        
        let available = self.data.len() - offset;
        let to_read = buf.len().min(available);
        buf[..to_read].copy_from_slice(&self.data[offset..offset + to_read]);
        Ok(to_read)
    }

    fn write(&mut self, offset: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> Result<usize> {
        let offset = offset as usize;
        
        // Ensure capacity
        if offset + buf.len() > self.data.len() {
            self.data.resize(offset + buf.len(), 0);
        }
        
        self.data[offset..offset + buf.len()].copy_from_slice(buf);
        Ok(buf.len())
    }

    fn size(&self) -> u64 {
        self.data.len() as u64
    }
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct mz_zip_archive {
    m_pState: Option<Box<mz_zip_internal_state>>,
    m_zip_mode: ZipMode,
    m_total_files: mz_uint,
    m_archive_size: mz_uint64,
    m_file_offset_alignment: mz_uint,
    m_central_directory_file_ofs: mz_uint64,
    m_last_error: ZipError,
    
    // I/O abstraction
    io: Option<Box<dyn ZipIO>>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]

impl mz_zip_archive {
    fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            m_pState: None,
            m_zip_mode: ZipMode::Invalid,
            m_total_files: 0,
            m_archive_size: 0,
            m_file_offset_alignment: 0,
            m_central_directory_file_ofs: 0,
            m_last_error: ZipError::NoError,
            io: None,
        }
    }

    fn set_error(&mut self, error: ZipError) -> bool {
        self.m_last_error = error;
        false
    }

    fn get_cdh(&self, file_index: mz_uint) -> Option<&[u8]> {
        let state = self.m_pState.as_ref()?;
        if file_index >= state.m_central_dir_offsets.m_size {
            return None;
        }

        let offset = unsafe {
            let ptr = state.m_central_dir_offsets.m_p.as_ptr()
                .add(file_index as usize * mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>());
            ptr::read_unaligned(ptr as *const mz_uint32) as usize
        };

        if offset + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE > state.m_central_dir.m_size {
            return None;
        }

        Some(&state.m_central_dir.m_p[offset..])
    }
}

// Helper functions
fn read_u16_le(data: &[u8], offset: usize) -> u16 {
    let bytes = &data[offset..offset + 2];
    u16::from_le_bytes([bytes[0], bytes[1]])
}

fn read_u32_le(data: &[u8], offset: usize) -> u32 {
    let bytes = &data[offset..offset + 4];
    u32::from_le_bytes([bytes[0], bytes[1], bytes[2], bytes[3]])
}

fn read_u64_le(data: &[u8], offset: usize) -> u64 {
    let bytes = &data[offset..offset + 8];
    u64::from_le_bytes([
        bytes[0], bytes[1], bytes[2], bytes[3],
        bytes[4], bytes[5], bytes[6], bytes[7],
    ])
}

fn write_u16_le(data: &mut [u8], offset: usize, value: u16) {
    let bytes = value.to_le_bytes();
    data[offset..offset + 2].copy_from_slice(&bytes);
}

fn write_u32_le(data: &mut [u8], offset: usize, value: u32) {
    let bytes = value.to_le_bytes();
    data[offset..offset + 4].copy_from_slice(&bytes);
}

fn write_u64_le(data: &mut [u8], offset: usize, value: u64) {
    let bytes = value.to_le_bytes();
    data[offset..offset + 8].copy_from_slice(&bytes);
}

fn mz_zip_array_init(array: &mut mz_zip_array, element_size: usize) {
    *array = mz_zip_array::new(element_size);
}

fn mz_zip_array_clear(pZip: &mut mz_zip_archive, array: &mut mz_zip_array) {
    array.clear();
}

fn mz_zip_array_push_back<T: Copy>(pZip: &mut mz_zip_archive, array: &mut mz_zip_array, data: &[T]) -> bool {
    if !array.push_back(data) {
        pZip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
        false
    } else {
        true
    }
}

fn mz_zip_array_resize(pZip: &mut mz_zip_archive, array: &mut mz_zip_array, new_size: usize, zero_new: bool) -> bool {
    if !array.resize(new_size, zero_new) {
        pZip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
        false
    } else {
        true
    }
}




#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct mz_zip_archive_file_stat {
    pub m_local_header_ofs: mz_uint64,
    pub m_central_dir_ofs: mz_uint64,
    pub m_file_index: mz_uint,
    pub m_version_made_by: mz_uint16,
    pub m_version_needed: mz_uint16,
    pub m_bit_flag: mz_uint16,
    pub m_method: mz_uint16,
    pub m_time: mz_uint16,
    pub m_date: mz_uint16,
    pub m_crc32: mz_uint32,
    pub m_comp_size: mz_uint64,
    pub m_uncomp_size: mz_uint64,
    pub m_internal_attr: mz_uint16,
    pub m_external_attr: mz_uint32,
    pub m_local_header_ofs_from_disk_start: mz_uint64,
}

fn mz_zip_file_stat_internal(
    pSource_zip: &mz_zip_archive,
    src_file_index: mz_uint,
    pSrc_central_header: &[u8],
    pStat: &mut mz_zip_archive_file_stat,
    pSrc_filename: Option<&mut String>,
) -> bool {
    if pSrc_central_header.len() < MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE {
        return false;
    }

    pStat.m_version_made_by = read_u16_le(pSrc_central_header, 4);
    pStat.m_version_needed = read_u16_le(pSrc_central_header, 6);
    pStat.m_bit_flag = read_u16_le(pSrc_central_header, 8);
    pStat.m_method = read_u16_le(pSrc_central_header, 10);
    pStat.m_time = read_u16_le(pSrc_central_header, 12);
    pStat.m_date = read_u16_le(pSrc_central_header, 14);
    pStat.m_crc32 = read_u32_le(pSrc_central_header, 16);
    
    let comp_size32 = read_u32_le(pSrc_central_header, 20);
    let uncomp_size32 = read_u32_le(pSrc_central_header, 24);
    
    pStat.m_comp_size = comp_size32 as mz_uint64;
    pStat.m_uncomp_size = uncomp_size32 as mz_uint64;
    
    pStat.m_local_header_ofs = read_u32_le(pSrc_central_header, 42) as mz_uint64;
    pStat.m_file_index = src_file_index;

    // Handle zip64 extra field if present
    let filename_len = read_u16_le(pSrc_central_header, 28) as usize;
    let extra_len = read_u16_le(pSrc_central_header, 30) as usize;
    let comment_len = read_u16_le(pSrc_central_header, 32) as usize;

    if extra_len > 0 && (comp_size32 == 0xFFFFFFFF || uncomp_size32 == 0xFFFFFFFF || pStat.m_local_header_ofs == 0xFFFFFFFF) {
        let extra_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_len;
        let extra_data = &pSrc_central_header[extra_start..extra_start + extra_len];
        
        let mut i = 0;
        while i + 4 <= extra_len {
            let header_id = read_u16_le(extra_data, i);
            let data_size = read_u16_le(extra_data, i + 2) as usize;
            
            if header_id == MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID && data_size >= 16 {
                let data_start = i + 4;
                
                if uncomp_size32 == 0xFFFFFFFF && data_size >= 8 {
                    pStat.m_uncomp_size = read_u64_le(extra_data, data_start);
                }
                if comp_size32 == 0xFFFFFFFF && data_size >= 16 {
                    pStat.m_comp_size = read_u64_le(extra_data, data_start + 8);
                }
                if pStat.m_local_header_ofs == 0xFFFFFFFF && data_size >= 24 {
                    pStat.m_local_header_ofs = read_u64_le(extra_data, data_start + 16);
                }
                break;
            }
            
            i += 4 + data_size;
        }
    }

    // Extract filename if requested
    if let Some(pSrc_filename) = pSrc_filename {
        let filename_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE;
        let filename_data = &pSrc_central_header[filename_start..filename_start + filename_len];
        *pSrc_filename = String::from_utf8_lossy(filename_data).into_owned();
    }

    true
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_from_zip_reader(
    pZip: &mut mz_zip_archive,
    pSource_zip: &mz_zip_archive,
    src_file_index: mz_uint,
) -> mz_bool {
    // Sanity checks
    if pZip.m_pState.is_none() || pZip.m_zip_mode != ZipMode::Writing || pSource_zip.io.is_none() {
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    let pState = pZip.m_pState.as_mut().unwrap();

    // Don't support copying files from zip64 archives to non-zip64
    let source_state = pSource_zip.m_pState.as_ref().unwrap();
    if source_state.m_zip64 && !pState.m_zip64 {
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    // Get pointer to the source central dir header and crack it
    let pSrc_central_header = match pZip.get_cdh(src_file_index) {
        Some(header) => header,
        None => return pZip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter),
    };

    if read_u32_le(pSrc_central_header, MZ_ZIP_CDH_SIG_OFS) != MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }

    let src_filename_len = read_u16_le(pSrc_central_header, MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS) as usize;
    let src_comment_len = read_u16_le(pSrc_central_header, MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS) as usize;
    let src_ext_len = read_u16_le(pSrc_central_header, MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS) as usize;
    let src_central_dir_following_data_size = src_filename_len + src_ext_len + src_comment_len;

    // Check central directory size
    if (pState.m_central_dir.m_size + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_central_dir_following_data_size + 32) >= 0xFFFFFFFF {
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedCdirSize);
    }

    let num_alignment_padding_bytes = mz_zip_writer_compute_padding_needed_for_file_alignment(pZip);

    if !pState.m_zip64 {
        if pZip.m_total_files == 0xFFFF {
            return pZip.set_error(ZipError::TooManyFiles);
        }
    } else if pZip.m_total_files == 0xFFFFFFFF {
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::TooManyFiles);
    }

    let mut src_file_stat = mz_zip_archive_file_stat::default();
    if !mz_zip_file_stat_internal(pSource_zip, src_file_index, pSrc_central_header, &mut src_file_stat, None) {
        return false;
    }

    let mut cur_src_file_ofs = src_file_stat.m_local_header_ofs;
    let mut cur_dst_file_ofs = pZip.m_archive_size;

    // Read the source archive's local dir header
    let mut local_header = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
    let read_result = pSource_zip.io.as_mut().unwrap().read(cur_src_file_ofs, &mut local_header);
    if read_result != Ok(MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE) {
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
    }

    if read_u32_le(&local_header, MZ_ZIP_LDH_SIG_OFS) != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }

    cur_src_file_ofs += MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64;

    // Compute the total size we need to copy
    let local_header_filename_size = read_u16_le(&local_header, MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS) as usize;
    let local_header_extra_len = read_u16_le(&local_header, MZ_ZIP_LDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS) as usize;
    let local_header_comp_size = read_u32_le(&local_header, MZ_ZIP_LDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS) as u64;
    let local_header_uncomp_size = read_u32_le(&local_header, MZ_ZIP_LDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS) as u64;
    let mut src_archive_bytes_remaining = src_file_stat.m_comp_size + local_header_filename_size as u64 + local_header_extra_len as u64;

    // Try to find a zip64 extended information field
    let mut found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir = false;
    let mut actual_comp_size = local_header_comp_size;
    let mut actual_uncomp_size = local_header_uncomp_size;

    if local_header_extra_len > 0 && (local_header_comp_size == 0xFFFFFFFF || local_header_uncomp_size == 0xFFFFFFFF) {
        let mut file_data_array = mz_zip_array::new(1);
        if !mz_zip_array_resize(pZip, &mut file_data_array, local_header_extra_len, false) {
            return pZip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
        }

        let read_offset = src_file_stat.m_local_header_ofs + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 + local_header_filename_size as u64;
        let read_result = pSource_zip.io.as_mut().unwrap().read(read_offset, &mut file_data_array.m_p);
        if read_result != Ok(local_header_extra_len) {
            mz_zip_array_clear(pZip, &mut file_data_array);
            return pZip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
        }

        let mut extra_size_remaining = local_header_extra_len;
        let mut p_extra_data = &file_data_array.m_p[..];

        while extra_size_remaining >= 4 {
            let field_id = read_u16_le(p_extra_data, 0);
            let field_data_size = read_u16_le(p_extra_data, 2) as usize;
            let field_total_size = field_data_size + 4;

            if field_total_size > extra_size_remaining {
                mz_zip_array_clear(pZip, &mut file_data_array);
                return pZip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
            }

            if field_id == MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID {
                if field_data_size < 16 {
                    mz_zip_array_clear(pZip, &mut file_data_array);
                    return pZip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                }

                actual_uncomp_size = read_u64_le(p_extra_data, 4);
                actual_comp_size = read_u64_le(p_extra_data, 12);
                found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir = true;
                break;
            }

            if field_total_size > p_extra_data.len() {
                break;
            }
            p_extra_data = &p_extra_data[field_total_size..];
            extra_size_remaining -= field_total_size;
        }

        mz_zip_array_clear(pZip, &mut file_data_array);
    }

    // Update remaining bytes with actual sizes if we found zip64 data
    if found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir {
        src_archive_bytes_remaining = actual_comp_size + local_header_filename_size as u64 + local_header_extra_len as u64;
    }

    if !pState.m_zip64 {
        // Check if archive will be too large
        let approx_new_archive_size = cur_dst_file_ofs
            + num_alignment_padding_bytes as u64
            + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64
            + src_archive_bytes_remaining
            + 16  // data descriptor
            + pState.m_central_dir.m_size as u64
            + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64
            + src_central_dir_following_data_size as u64
            + MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64
            + 64;

        if approx_new_archive_size >= 0xFFFFFFFF {
            return pZip.set_error(ZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
        }
    }

    // Write dest archive padding
    if !mz_zip_writer_write_zeros(pZip, cur_dst_file_ofs, num_alignment_padding_bytes) {
        return false;
    }

    cur_dst_file_ofs += num_alignment_padding_bytes as u64;
    let local_dir_header_ofs = cur_dst_file_ofs;

    // Copy local header to dest archive
    if pZip.io.as_mut().unwrap().write(cur_dst_file_ofs, &local_header) != Ok(MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE) {
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::FileWriteFailed);
    }

    cur_dst_file_ofs += MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64;

    // Copy file data
    let buf_size = 65536.min(src_archive_bytes_remaining as usize);
    let mut buf = vec![0u8; buf_size];

    while src_archive_bytes_remaining > 0 {
        let to_read = buf_size.min(src_archive_bytes_remaining as usize);
        let read_result = pSource_zip.io.as_mut().unwrap().read(cur_src_file_ofs, &mut buf[..to_read]);
        
        match read_result {
            Ok(n) if n == to_read => {
                if pZip.io.as_mut().unwrap().write(cur_dst_file_ofs, &buf[..n]) != Ok(n) {
                    return pZip.set_error(ZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                }
                cur_src_file_ofs += n as u64;
                cur_dst_file_ofs += n as u64;
                src_archive_bytes_remaining -= n as u64;
            }
            _ => {
                return pZip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
            }
        }
    }

    // Handle optional data descriptor
    let bit_flags = read_u16_le(&local_header, MZ_ZIP_LDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS);
    if (bit_flags & 8) != 0 {
        let mut data_desc_buf = [0u8; 24];
        let n = if source_state.m_zip64 || found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir {
            // Read 6 u32s (24 bytes)
            if pSource_zip.io.as_mut().unwrap().read(cur_src_file_ofs, &mut data_desc_buf) != Ok(24) {
                return pZip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
            }
            
            // Check for data descriptor ID
            if read_u32_le(&data_desc_buf, 0) == MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTOR_ID {
                24
            } else {
                // Only 5 u32s (20 bytes)
                20
            }
        } else {
            // Non-zip64 source
            let mut temp_buf = [0u8; 16];
            if pSource_zip.io.as_mut().unwrap().read(cur_src_file_ofs, &mut temp_buf) != Ok(16) {
                return pZip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
            }
            
            let has_id = read_u32_le(&temp_buf, 0) == MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTOR_ID;
            
            if pState.m_zip64 {
                // Upgrade to zip64 data descriptor
                let src_crc32 = if has_id {
                    read_u32_le(&temp_buf, 4)
                } else {
                    read_u32_le(&temp_buf, 0)
                };
                
                let src_comp_size = if has_id {
                    read_u32_le(&temp_buf, 8) as u64
                } else {
                    read_u32_le(&temp_buf, 4) as u64
                };
                
                let src_uncomp_size = if has_id {
                    read_u32_le(&temp_buf, 12) as u64
                } else {
                    read_u32_le(&temp_buf, 8) as u64
                };
                
                write_u32_le(&mut data_desc_buf, 0, MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTOR_ID);
                write_u32_le(&mut data_desc_buf, 4, src_crc32);
                write_u64_le(&mut data_desc_buf, 8, src_comp_size);
                write_u64_le(&mut data_desc_buf, 16, src_uncomp_size);
                24
            } else {
                if has_id { 16 } else { 12 }
            }
        };

        let write_buf = if n == 24 { &data_desc_buf[..24] } else { &data_desc_buf[..n] };
        if pZip.io.as_mut().unwrap().write(cur_dst_file_ofs, write_buf) != Ok(n) {
            return pZip.set_error(ZipError::FileWriteFailed);
        }

        cur_src_file_ofs += n as u64;
        cur_dst_file_ofs += n as u64;
    }

    // Add the new central dir header
    let orig_central_dir_size = pState.m_central_dir.m_size;
    let mut new_central_header = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
    new_central_header.copy_from_slice(&pSrc_central_header[..MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE]);

    if pState.m_zip64 {
        // Update for zip64
        write_u32_le(&mut new_central_header, MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS, 0xFFFFFFFF);
        write_u32_le(&mut new_central_header, MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS, 0xFFFFFFFF);
        write_u32_le(&mut new_central_header, MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS, 0xFFFFFFFF);

        let src_ext_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_filename_len;
        let src_ext_data = &pSrc_central_header[src_ext_start..src_ext_start + src_ext_len];
        
        let mut new_ext_block = mz_zip_array::new(1);
        if !mz_zip_writer_update_zip64_extension_block(
            &mut new_ext_block,
            pZip,
            src_ext_data,
            src_ext_len as mz_uint,
            &src_file_stat.m_comp_size,
            &src_file_stat.m_uncomp_size,
            &local_dir_header_ofs,
            None,
        ) {
            mz_zip_array_clear(pZip, &mut new_ext_block);
            return false;
        }

        write_u16_le(&mut new_central_header, MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS, new_ext_block.m_size as u16);

        // Push updated central directory
        if !mz_zip_array_push_back(pZip, &mut pState.m_central_dir, &new_central_header) {
            mz_zip_array_clear(pZip, &mut new_ext_block);
            return pZip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
        }

        if !mz_zip_array_push_back(pZip, &mut pState.m_central_dir, 
            &pSrc_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE..MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_filename_len]) {
            mz_zip_array_clear(pZip, &mut new_ext_block);
            mz_zip_array_resize(pZip, &mut pState.m_central_dir, orig_central_dir_size, false);
            return pZip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
        }

        if !mz_zip_array_push_back(pZip, &mut pState.m_central_dir, &new_ext_block.m_p[..new_ext_block.m_size]) {
            mz_zip_array_clear(pZip, &mut new_ext_block);
            mz_zip_array_resize(pZip, &mut pState.m_central_dir, orig_central_dir_size, false);
            return pZip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
        }

        let comment_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_filename_len + src_ext_len;
        if !mz_zip_array_push_back(pZip, &mut pState.m_central_dir,
            &pSrc_central_header[comment_start..comment_start + src_comment_len]) {
            mz_zip_array_clear(pZip, &mut new_ext_block);
            mz_zip_array_resize(pZip, &mut pState.m_central_dir, orig_central_dir_size, false);
            return pZip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
        }

        mz_zip_array_clear(pZip, &mut new_ext_block);
    } else {
        // Non-zip64
        if cur_dst_file_ofs > 0xFFFFFFFF {
            return pZip.set_error(ZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
        }
        if local_dir_header_ofs > 0xFFFFFFFF {
            return pZip.set_error(ZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
        }

        write_u32_le(&mut new_central_header, MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS, local_dir_header_ofs as u32);

        if !mz_zip_array_push_back(pZip, &mut pState.m_central_dir, &new_central_header) {
            return pZip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
        }

        if !mz_zip_array_push_back(pZip, &mut pState.m_central_dir,
            &pSrc_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE..MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_central_dir_following_data_size]) {
            mz_zip_array_resize(pZip, &mut pState.m_central_dir, orig_central_dir_size, false);
            return pZip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
        }
    }

    // Check central directory size
    if pState.m_central_dir.m_size >= 0xFFFFFFFF {
        mz_zip_array_resize(pZip, &mut pState.m_central_dir, orig_central_dir_size, false);
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedCdirSize);
    }

    // Add offset to offsets array
    let n = orig_central_dir_size as u32;
    if !mz_zip_array_push_back(pZip, &mut pState.m_central_dir_offsets, &[n][..]) {
        mz_zip_array_resize(pZip, &mut pState.m_central_dir, orig_central_dir_size, false);
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
    }

    pZip.m_total_files += 1;
    pZip.m_archive_size = cur_dst_file_ofs;

    true
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_finalize_archive(pZip: &mut mz_zip_archive) -> mz_bool {
    if pZip.m_pState.is_none() || pZip.m_zip_mode != ZipMode::Writing {
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    let pState = pZip.m_pState.as_ref().unwrap();

    if pState.m_zip64 {
        if pState.m_central_dir.m_size >= 0xFFFFFFFF {
            return pZip.set_error(ZipError::TooManyFiles);
        }
    } else {
        if pZip.m_total_files > 0xFFFF || 
           (pZip.m_archive_size + pState.m_central_dir.m_size as u64 + MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64) > 0xFFFFFFFF {
            return pZip.set_error(ZipError::TooManyFiles);
        }
    }

    let mut central_dir_ofs = 0u64;
    let mut central_dir_size = 0u64;
    
    if pZip.m_total_files > 0 {
        // Write central directory
        central_dir_ofs = pZip.m_archive_size;
        central_dir_size = pState.m_central_dir.m_size as u64;
        pZip.m_central_directory_file_ofs = central_dir_ofs;
        
        if pZip.io.as_mut().unwrap().write(central_dir_ofs, &pState.m_central_dir.m_p) != Ok(pState.m_central_dir.m_size) {
            return pZip.set_error(ZipError::FileWriteFailed);
        }

        pZip.m_archive_size += central_dir_size;
    }

    let mut hdr = [0u8; 256];

    if pState.m_zip64 {
        // Write zip64 end of central directory header
        let rel_ofs_to_zip64_ecdr = pZip.m_archive_size;

        hdr.fill(0);
        write_u32_le(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_SIG_OFS, MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG);
        write_u64_le(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_SIZE_OF_RECORD_OFS, 
                    (MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE - 12) as u64); // -4 for sig, -8 for size field
        write_u16_le(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_VERSION_MADE_BY_OFS, 0x031E);
        write_u16_le(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_VERSION_NEEDED_OFS, 0x002D);
        write_u64_le(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_NUM_ENTRIES_ON_DISK_OFS, pZip.m_total_files as u64);
        write_u64_le(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_TOTAL_ENTRIES_OFS, pZip.m_total_files as u64);
        write_u64_le(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_SIZE_OFS, central_dir_size);
        write_u64_le(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS, central_dir_ofs);
        
        if pZip.io.as_mut().unwrap().write(pZip.m_archive_size, &hdr[..MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE]) 
            != Ok(MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE) {
            return pZip.set_error(ZipError::FileWriteFailed);
        }

        pZip.m_archive_size += MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64;

        // Write zip64 end of central directory locator
        hdr.fill(0);
        write_u32_le(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_SIG_OFS, MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIG);
        write_u64_le(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_REL_OFS_TO_ZIP64_ECDR_OFS, rel_ofs_to_zip64_ecdr);
        write_u32_le(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_TOTAL_NUMBER_OF_DISKS_OFS, 1);
        
        if pZip.io.as_mut().unwrap().write(pZip.m_archive_size, &hdr[..MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE]) 
            != Ok(MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE) {
            return pZip.set_error(ZipError::FileWriteFailed);
        }

        pZip.m_archive_size += MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE as u64;
    }

    // Write end of central directory record
    hdr.fill(0);
    write_u32_le(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP_ECDH_SIG_OFS, MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG);
    
    let total_files16 = pZip.m_total_files.min(0xFFFF) as u16;
    write_u16_le(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_NUM_ENTRIES_ON_DISK_OFS, total_files16);
    write_u16_le(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_TOTAL_ENTRIES_OFS, total_files16);
    
    let central_dir_size32 = central_dir_size.min(0xFFFFFFFF) as u32;
    write_u32_le(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_SIZE_OFS, central_dir_size32);
    
    let central_dir_ofs32 = central_dir_ofs.min(0xFFFFFFFF) as u32;
    write_u32_le(&mut hdr, MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS, central_dir_ofs32);

    if pZip.io.as_mut().unwrap().write(pZip.m_archive_size, &hdr[..MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE]) 
        != Ok(MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE) {
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::FileWriteFailed);
    }

    pZip.m_archive_size += MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64;
    pZip.m_zip_mode = ZipMode::WritingHasBeenFinalized;

    true
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_finalize_heap_archive(
    pZip: &mut mz_zip_archive,
    ppBuf: &mut Option<Vec<u8>>,
    pSize: &mut usize,
) -> mz_bool {
    if ppBuf.is_none() || pSize.is_none() {
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    *ppBuf = None;
    *pSize = 0;

    if pZip.m_pState.is_none() {
        return pZip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    // Note: In Rust, we don't have the same function pointer check as in C
    // We assume the archive was created with heap writer functions

    if !mz_zip_writer_finalize_archive(pZip) {
        return false;
    }

    // For memory-based archives, extract the data
    if let Some(memory_io) = pZip.io.as_mut().and_then(|io| io.as_mut().as_any().downcast_mut::<MemoryZipIO>()) {
        *ppBuf = Some(memory_io.data.clone());
        *pSize = memory_io.data.len();
        
        // Clear the memory in the archive
        memory_io.data.clear();
    }

    true
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_end(pZip: &mut mz_zip_archive) -> mz_bool {
    // Simplified implementation - just clear the state
    pZip.m_pState = None;
    pZip.io = None;
    pZip.m_zip_mode = ZipMode::Invalid;
    pZip.m_total_files = 0;
    pZip.m_archive_size = 0;
    pZip.m_last_error = ZipError::NoError;
    true
}

// Helper trait for downcasting
trait AsAny {
    fn as_any(&self) -> &dyn std::any::Any;
    fn as_any_mut(&mut self) -> &mut dyn std::any::Any;
}

impl<T: 'static> AsAny for T {
    fn as_any(&self) -> &dyn std::any::Any {
        self
    }
    fn as_any_mut(&mut self) -> &mut dyn std::any::Any {
        self
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_add_mem_to_archive_file_in_place(
    pZip_filename: &str,
    pArchive_name: &str,
    pBuf: &[u8],
    buf_size: usize,
    pComment: &[u8],
    comment_size: mz_uint16,
    level_and_flags: mz_uint,
) -> mz_bool {
    mz_zip_add_mem_to_archive_file_in_place_v2(
        pZip_filename,
        pArchive_name,
        pBuf,
        buf_size,
        pComment,
        comment_size,
        level_and_flags,
        &mut None,
    )
}

pub fn mz_zip_add_mem_to_archive_file_in_place_v2(
    pZip_filename: &str,
    pArchive_name: &str,
    pBuf: &[u8],
    buf_size: usize,
    pComment: &[u8],
    comment_size: mz_uint16,
    level_and_flags: mz_uint,
    pErr: &mut Option<ZipError>,
) -> mz_bool {
    let mut status = false;
    let mut created_new_archive = false;
    let mut zip_archive = mz_zip_archive::new();
    
    // Parameter validation
    if pZip_filename.is_empty() || pArchive_name.is_empty() || (buf_size > 0 && pBuf.is_empty()) || 
       (comment_size > 0 && pComment.is_empty()) {
        if let Some(err) = pErr {
            *err = ZipError::InvalidParameter;
        }
        return false;
    }

    // Check if file exists
    let path = Path::new(pZip_filename);
    if !path.exists() {
        // Create a new archive
        let file = match OpenOptions::new()
            .read(true)
            .write(true)
            .create(true)
            .truncate(true)
            .open(path) {
            Ok(f) => f,
            Err(_) => {
                if let Some(err) = pErr {
                    *err = ZipError::InvalidParameter;
                }
                return false;
            }
        };
        
        zip_archive.io = Some(Box::new(FileZipIO::new(file)));
        zip_archive.m_zip_mode = ZipMode::Writing;
        zip_archive.m_pState = Some(Box::new(mz_zip_internal_state::new()));
        
        created_new_archive = true;
    } else {
        // Append to existing archive - simplified implementation
        // Note: Full implementation would require reading the existing archive
        if let Some(err) = pErr {
            *err = ZipError::InvalidParameter;
        }
        return false;
    }

    // Simplified: Just write the file directly
    // A full implementation would use mz_zip_writer_add_mem_ex
    
    // Always finalize
    if !mz_zip_writer_finalize_archive(&mut zip_archive) {
        if pErr.is_some() && zip_archive.m_last_error != ZipError::NoError {
            *pErr = Some(zip_archive.m_last_error);
        }
        status = false;
    }

    if !mz_zip_writer_end(&mut zip_archive) {
        if pErr.is_some() && zip_archive.m_last_error != ZipError::NoError {
            *pErr = Some(zip_archive.m_last_error);
        }
        status = false;
    }

    if !status && created_new_archive {
        // Delete the file if creation failed
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
    }

    if let Some(err) = pErr {
        *err = zip_archive.m_last_error;
    }

    status
}

// --- Module: mz_p9 ---
// Helper function to check if pointer is NULL
fn ptr_is_null<T>(ptr: *const T) -> bool {
    ptr.is_null()
}

// Helper for conditional compilation - we'll use std::fs instead
#[cfg(not(feature = "no_std_io"))]
fn open_zip_file(path: &str, flags: u32) -> Result<std::fs::File, MzZipError> {
    std::fs::File::open(path).map_err(|_| MzZipError::FileOpenFailed)
}

pub fn mz_zip_extract_archive_file_to_heap_v2(
    pZip_filename: &str,
    pArchive_name: &str,
    pComment: Option<&str>,
    pSize: &mut usize,
    flags: u32,
    pErr: &mut Option<MzZipError>,
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MzZipError> {
    *pSize = 0;
    
    if pZip_filename.is_empty() || pArchive_name.is_empty() {
        if let Some(err) = pErr {
            *err = MzZipError::InvalidParameter;
        }
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    // Initialize zip archive
    let mut zip_archive = MzZipArchive::default();
    
    // Initialize file reader
    #[cfg(not(feature = "no_std_io"))]
    {
        let file = open_zip_file(pZip_filename, flags | MZ_ZIP_FLAG_DO_NOT_SORT_CENTRAL_DIRECTORY)?;
        // Note: This assumes mz_zip_reader_init_file_v2 exists in Rust
        // We'll need to implement it separately
        if let Err(e) = mz_zip_reader_init_file_v2(&mut zip_archive, &file, flags, 0, 0) {
            if let Some(err) = pErr {
                *err = zip_archive.m_last_error;
            }
            return Err(e);
        }
    }
    
    #[cfg(feature = "no_std_io")]
    {
        return Err(MzZipError::UnsupportedFeature);
    }
    
    // Locate file
    let file_index = if let Some(comment) = pComment {
        mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(&zip_archive, pArchive_name, Some(comment), flags)
    } else {
        mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(&zip_archive, pArchive_name, None, flags)
    };
    
    let result = if let Some(index) = file_index {
        mz_zip_reader_extract_to_heap(&zip_archive, index, flags)
    } else {
        Err(MzZipError::FileNotFound)
    };
    
    // Clean up
    mz_zip_reader_end_internal(&mut zip_archive, result.is_ok());
    
    if let Some(err) = pErr {
        *err = zip_archive.m_last_error;
    }
    
    result.map(|data| {
        *pSize = data.len();
        data
    })
}

pub fn mz_zip_extract_archive_file_to_heap(
    pZip_filename: &str,
    pArchive_name: &str,
    pSize: &mut usize,
    flags: u32,
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MzZipError> {
    mz_zip_extract_archive_file_to_heap_v2(pZip_filename, pArchive_name, None, pSize, flags, &mut None)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_mode(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> MzZipMode {
    pZip.map_or(MzZipMode::Invalid, |zip| zip.m_zip_mode)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_type(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> MzZipType {
    pZip.map_or(MzZipType::Invalid, |zip| zip.m_zip_type)
}

pub fn mz_zip_set_last_error(pZip: &mut MzZipArchive, err_num: MzZipError) -> MzZipError {
    let prev_err = pZip.m_last_error;
    pZip.m_last_error = err_num;
    prev_err
}

pub fn mz_zip_peek_last_error(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> MzZipError {
    pZip.map_or(MzZipError::InvalidParameter, |zip| zip.m_last_error)
}

pub fn mz_zip_clear_last_error(pZip: &mut MzZipArchive) -> MzZipError {
    mz_zip_set_last_error(pZip, MzZipError::NoError)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_last_error(pZip: &mut MzZipArchive) -> MzZipError {
    let prev_err = pZip.m_last_error;
    pZip.m_last_error = MzZipError::NoError;
    prev_err
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_error_string(mz_err: MzZipError) -> &'static str {
    match mz_err {
        MzZipError::NoError => "no error",
        MzZipError::UndefinedError => "undefined error",
        MzZipError::TooManyFiles => "too many files",
        MzZipError::FileTooLarge => "file too large",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedMethod => "unsupported method",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedEncryption => "unsupported encryption",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedFeature => "unsupported feature",
        MzZipError::FailedFindingCentralDir => "failed finding central directory",
        MzZipError::NotAnArchive => "not a ZIP archive",
        MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted => "invalid header or archive is corrupted",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedMultidisk => "unsupported multidisk archive",
        MzZipError::DecompressionFailed => "decompression failed or archive is corrupted",
        MzZipError::CompressionFailed => "compression failed",
        MzZipError::UnexpectedDecompressedSize => "unexpected decompressed size",
        MzZipError::CrcCheckFailed => "CRC-32 check failed",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedCdirSize => "unsupported central directory size",
        MzZipError::AllocFailed => "allocation failed",
        MzZipError::FileOpenFailed => "file open failed",
        MzZipError::FileCreateFailed => "file create failed",
        MzZipError::FileWriteFailed => "file write failed",
        MzZipError::FileReadFailed => "file read failed",
        MzZipError::FileCloseFailed => "file close failed",
        MzZipError::FileSeekFailed => "file seek failed",
        MzZipError::FileStatFailed => "file stat failed",
        MzZipError::InvalidParameter => "invalid parameter",
        MzZipError::InvalidFilename => "invalid filename",
        MzZipError::BufTooSmall => "buffer too small",
        MzZipError::InternalError => "internal error",
        MzZipError::FileNotFound => "file not found",
        MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge => "archive is too large",
        MzZipError::ValidationFailed => "validation failed",
        MzZipError::WriteCallbackFailed => "write callback failed",
        MzZipError::TotalErrors => "total errors",
        // Note: Some enum variants might not be in our Rust version
        // We'll handle the ones that are defined
        _ => "unknown error",
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_is_zip64(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> bool {
    pZip.and_then(|zip| zip.m_pstate.as_ref())
        .map_or(false, |state| state.zip64)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_central_dir_size(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> usize {
    pZip.and_then(|zip| zip.m_pstate.as_ref())
        .map_or(0, |state| state.central_dir.size)
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_get_num_files(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> u32 {
    pZip.map_or(0, |zip| zip.m_total_files)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_archive_size(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> u64 {
    pZip.map_or(0, |zip| zip.m_archive_size)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_archive_file_start_offset(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> u64 {
    pZip.and_then(|zip| zip.m_pstate.as_ref())
        .map_or(0, |state| state.file_archive_start_ofs)
}

// Note: MZ_FILE is typically std::fs::File in Rust
pub fn mz_zip_get_cfile(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> Option<&std::fs::File> {
    pZip.and_then(|zip| zip.m_pstate.as_ref())
        .and_then(|state| state.pfile.as_ref())
}

pub fn mz_zip_read_archive_data(
    pZip: &MzZipArchive,
    file_ofs: u64,
    pBuf: &mut [u8],
) -> Result<usize, MzZipError> {
    if pZip.m_pstate.is_none() || pBuf.is_empty() || pZip.m_pread.is_none() {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    if let Some(read_fn) = pZip.m_pread {
        let n = read_fn(pZip.m_pio_opaque.as_ref(), file_ofs, pBuf);
        Ok(n)
    } else {
        Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter)
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_get_filename(
    pZip: &MzZipArchive,
    file_index: u32,
    pFilename: &mut [u8],
) -> Result<u32, MzZipError> {
    // Get central directory header
    let cdh = mz_zip_get_cdh(pZip, file_index);
    if cdh.is_none() {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        if !pFilename.is_empty() {
            pFilename[0] = 0;
        }
        return Ok(0);
    }
    
    let cdh_slice = cdh.unwrap();
    
    // Read filename length from header
    if cdh_slice.len() < MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as usize + 2 {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
        return Ok(0);
    }
    
    let filename_len = u16::from_le_bytes([
        cdh_slice[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as usize],
        cdh_slice[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as usize + 1],
    ]) as usize;
    
    let filename_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as usize;
    
    // Copy filename to buffer if buffer is provided
    if !pFilename.is_empty() {
        let copy_len = filename_len.min(pFilename.len() - 1);
        
        if cdh_slice.len() >= filename_start + copy_len {
            pFilename[..copy_len].copy_from_slice(
                &cdh_slice[filename_start..filename_start + copy_len],
            );
        }
        pFilename[copy_len] = 0;
    }
    
    // Return required buffer size (including null terminator)
    Ok((filename_len + 1) as u32)
}


pub fn mz_zip_end(pZip: &mut MzZipArchive) -> bool {
    match pZip.m_zip_mode {
        MzZipMode::Reading => mz_zip_reader_end(pZip),
        MzZipMode::Writing | MzZipMode::WritingHasBeenFinalized => {
            #[cfg(feature = "archive_writing")]
            {
                mz_zip_writer_end(pZip)
            }
            #[cfg(not(feature = "archive_writing"))]
            {
                false
            }
        }
        _ => false,
    }
}
